      boneTransforms[rid] = computeBoneRigidTransform(pRest, cRest, pCur, cCur);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const rx = meshRestPos[i*3], ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let px = 0, py = 0, pz = 0;
      let totalW = 0;

      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        const w   = entries[e + 1];
        const bt  = boneTransforms[rid];
        if (!bt) continue;
        const R = bt.R, t = bt.t;
        px += w * (R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + t[0]);
        py += w * (R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + t[1]);
        pz += w * (R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + t[2]);
        totalW += w;
      }

      if (totalW > 1e-6) {
        outPos[i*3]   = px / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+1] = py / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+2] = pz / totalW;
      } else {
        outPos[i*3] = rx; outPos[i*3+1] = ry; outPos[i*3+2] = rz;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Compute comparison heatmap colors: cage-deformed vs bone-skinned reference
  function computeComparisonColors(deformedPos, refPos, colors) {
    if (!colors) return;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const regionNames = {0:'torso',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
      5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
      11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis'};

    // Per-region error stats (for one-shot diagnostic)
    const regionStats = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const dx = deformedPos[i*3] - refPos[i*3];
      const dy = deformedPos[i*3+1] - refPos[i*3+1];
      const dz = deformedPos[i*3+2] - refPos[i*3+2];
      const err = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);

      // Get dominant region
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        if (entries[e+1] > domW) { domRid = entries[e]; domW = entries[e+1]; }
      }
      if (!regionStats[domRid]) regionStats[domRid] = { sum: 0, count: 0, max: 0, maxVert: 0 };
      regionStats[domRid].sum += err;
      regionStats[domRid].count++;
      if (err > regionStats[domRid].max) { regionStats[domRid].max = err; regionStats[domRid].maxVert = i; }

      // Color: <1mm green, 1-5mm green→yellow, 5-15mm yellow→red, >15mm red
      const errMM = err * 1000; // convert to mm (assuming model units = meters)
      let r, g, b;
      if (errMM < 1) {
        r = 0; g = 0.8; b = 0;
      } else if (errMM < 5) {
        const t = (errMM - 1) / 4;
        r = t; g = 0.8; b = 0;
      } else if (errMM < 15) {
        const t = (errMM - 5) / 10;
        r = 1; g = 0.8 * (1 - t); b = 0;
      } else {
        r = 1; g = 0; b = 0;
      }
      colors[i*3] = r; colors[i*3+1] = g; colors[i*3+2] = b;
    }

    // One-shot diagnostic log
    if (!window._boneCompDiagDone) {
      window._boneCompDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== BONE vs CAGE COMPARISON ===');
      let overallSum = 0, overallCount = 0, worstRegion = '', worstMax = 0;
      const sorted = Object.entries(regionStats).sort((a,b) => b[1].max - a[1].max);
      for (const [rid, s] of sorted) {
        const avg = (s.sum / s.count * 1000).toFixed(1);
        const max = (s.max * 1000).toFixed(1);
        const name = regionNames[rid] || rid;
        console.log('Region ' + rid + ' (' + name + '): avg ' + avg + 'mm, max ' + max + 'mm (vert ' + s.maxVert + ')');
        overallSum += s.sum;
        overallCount += s.count;
        if (s.max > worstMax) { worstMax = s.max; worstRegion = name; }
      }
      const overallAvg = (overallSum / overallCount * 1000).toFixed(1);
      console.log('Overall: avg ' + overallAvg + 'mm | Worst region: ' + worstRegion + ' (' + (worstMax * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm max)');
    }
  }

  function deformMesh(){
    if (!meshVertRegions) return;
    const alpha = +$('alpha').value;
    const pos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Debug region coloring / strain / bone error visualization
    const debugRegions = $('debugRegions').checked;
    const showStrainCheck = $('showStrain').checked;
    const showBoneError = $('showBoneError').checked;
    const showSurfaceHealth = $('showSurfaceHealth').checked;
    const useVertexColors = debugRegions || showStrainCheck || showBoneError || showSurfaceHealth;
    const colors = meshGeo.attributes.color ? meshGeo.attributes.color.array : null;
    if (meshObj) {
      meshObj.material.vertexColors = useVertexColors;
      // When debug regions are on, hide the texture so vertex colors show as
      // pure flat colors. Otherwise the texture multiplies with vertex colors,
      // making it impossible to distinguish regions on textured meshes.
      if (useVertexColors && meshObj.material.map) {
        meshObj.material._savedMap = meshObj.material.map;
        meshObj.material.map = null;
      } else if (!useVertexColors && meshObj.material._savedMap) {
        meshObj.material.map = meshObj.material._savedMap;
        delete meshObj.material._savedMap;
      }
      meshObj.material.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    // Compute best-fit rigid transform per region from current cage state
    let transforms = computeAllRegionTransforms();
    // Temporal smoothing DISABLED: independently smoothing each region's transform
    // breaks the property that neighboring regions agree at shared joint boundaries,
    // which directly causes seam gaps (visible holes) at elbows/shoulders/knees.
    // transforms = smoothTransformsTemporal(transforms);
    lastRegionTransforms = transforms;

    // One-shot diagnostic: dump all Kabsch transforms
    if (window._posDiagDone && !window._kabschDiagDone) {
      window._kabschDiagDone = true;
      const posMode = armState && armState.positionBased;
      console.log('=== REGION TRANSFORMS (posMode=' + posMode + ', arms=analytical) ===');
      const regionNames = {0:'torso',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis'};
      for (let r = 0; r <= 14; r++) {
        const tf = transforms[r];
        if (!tf) { console.log('  ' + (regionNames[r]||r) + ': MISSING'); continue; }
        const det = mat3_det(tf.R);
        const tMag = Math.sqrt(tf.t[0]*tf.t[0]+tf.t[1]*tf.t[1]+tf.t[2]*tf.t[2]);
        const hasNaN = Array.from(tf.R).some(v=>isNaN(v)) || tf.t.some(v=>isNaN(v));
        console.log('  ' + (regionNames[r]||r) + ': det=' + det.toFixed(4) + ' |t|=' + tMag.toFixed(4) + ' t=[' + tf.t.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']' + (hasNaN?' NaN!':''));
      }
    }

    const useDQS = $('useDQS').checked;
    const hardRegions = $('hardRegions').checked;

    // Adaptive shoulder blend: compute arm-vs-torso angular difference per side.
    // When the arm swing is large (>45°), reduce the torso weight contribution for
    // vertices blended between torso and arm to prevent LBS inversion artifacts.
    // armTorsoFade[0] = left arm fade, armTorsoFade[1] = right arm fade.
    // 1.0 = full torso weight (small arm swing), 0.0 = zero torso weight (extreme arm swing).
    const armTorsoFade = [1.0, 1.0];
    if (transforms[0]) {
      for (let side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
        const armRid = side === 0 ? 7 : 8; // l_uarm / r_uarm
        const Ra = transforms[armRid] ? transforms[armRid].R : null;
        const Rt = transforms[0].R;
        if (!Ra) continue;
        // Frobenius inner product = trace(Ra * Rt^T) = 1 + 2*cos(angle)
        const dot = Rt[0]*Ra[0]+Rt[1]*Ra[1]+Rt[2]*Ra[2]+Rt[3]*Ra[3]+Rt[4]*Ra[4]+Rt[5]*Ra[5]+Rt[6]*Ra[6]+Rt[7]*Ra[7]+Rt[8]*Ra[8];
        const angle = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (dot - 1) / 2)));
        const FADE_START = 0.78; // ~45° - start reducing torso weight
        const FADE_END = 2.35;   // ~135° - torso weight = 0
        if (angle > FADE_START) {
          armTorsoFade[side] = Math.max(0, 1 - (angle - FADE_START) / (FADE_END - FADE_START));
        }
      }
    }
    _armTorsoFade[0] = armTorsoFade[0];
    _armTorsoFade[1] = armTorsoFade[1];

    // Pre-convert each region's (R, t) to dual quaternion once per frame
    // Always computed (not just DQS mode) because hybrid arm DQS uses them in LBS mode
    const regionDQs = {};
    if (!hardRegions) {
      for (const rid in transforms) {
        const tf = transforms[rid];
        if (!tf) continue;
        const q = mat3_to_quat(tf.R);
        regionDQs[rid] = rigid_to_dq(q, tf.t);
      }
    }

    // Precompute spine interpolation data: slerp between pelvis and torso transforms
    // so torso mesh vertices get smooth non-rigid spine bending instead of one rigid average.
    let spineInterp = null;
    const tfPelvis = transforms[14], tfTorso = transforms[0];
    if (tfPelvis && tfTorso && P.pelvis && P.neck) {
      const pelvisY = P.pelvis[1];
      const neckY = P.neck[1];
      const spineHeight = neckY - pelvisY;
      if (spineHeight > 0.01) {
        spineInterp = {
          pelvisQ: mat3_to_quat(tfPelvis.R), pelvisT: tfPelvis.t,
          torsoQ: mat3_to_quat(tfTorso.R), torsoT: tfTorso.t,
          pelvisY, neckY, invHeight: 1.0 / spineHeight,
          // Cache: pre-compute slerp results for 5 height bands to avoid per-vertex slerp
          bands: null
        };
        // Pre-compute 5 interpolation bands (t = 0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
        const bands = [];
        for (let b = 0; b <= 4; b++) {
          const t = b / 4;
          const q = quat_slerp(spineInterp.pelvisQ, spineInterp.torsoQ, t);
          const R = quat_to_mat3(q);
          const tx = spineInterp.pelvisT[0] + t * (spineInterp.torsoT[0] - spineInterp.pelvisT[0]);
          const ty = spineInterp.pelvisT[1] + t * (spineInterp.torsoT[1] - spineInterp.pelvisT[1]);
          const tz = spineInterp.pelvisT[2] + t * (spineInterp.torsoT[2] - spineInterp.pelvisT[2]);
          bands.push({ R, t: [tx, ty, tz] });
        }
        spineInterp.bands = bands;
      }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const rx = meshRestPos[i*3];
      const ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1];
      const rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];

      const entries = meshVertRegions[i]; // flat pairs: [rid, w, rid, w, ...]
      let dominantRid = 0, dominantW = -1;
      // Find dominant region first (needed for hard mode and debug color)
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        if (entries[e + 1] > dominantW) { dominantRid = entries[e]; dominantW = entries[e + 1]; }
      }
      let px, py, pz;

      if (hardRegions) {
        // --- Hard assignment: dominant region only, zero blending ---
        const tf = transforms[dominantRid];
        if (tf) {
          const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
          px = R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + t[0];
          py = R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + t[1];
          pz = R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + t[2];
        } else { px = rx; py = ry; pz = rz; }
      } else if (useDQS) {
        // --- Standard DQS blend for all verts ---
        let bqr0 = 0, bqr1 = 0, bqr2 = 0, bqr3 = 0;
        let bqd0 = 0, bqd1 = 0, bqd2 = 0, bqd3 = 0;
        let firstQr = null;

        for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
          const rid = entries[e];
          const w   = entries[e + 1];
          const dq  = regionDQs[rid];
          if (!dq) continue;
          let qr = dq.qr, qd = dq.qd;

          if (!firstQr) { firstQr = qr; }
          else {
            const dot = firstQr[0]*qr[0] + firstQr[1]*qr[1] + firstQr[2]*qr[2] + firstQr[3]*qr[3];
            if (dot < 0) {
              qr = [-qr[0], -qr[1], -qr[2], -qr[3]];
              qd = [-qd[0], -qd[1], -qd[2], -qd[3]];
            }
          }

          bqr0 += w * qr[0]; bqr1 += w * qr[1]; bqr2 += w * qr[2]; bqr3 += w * qr[3];
          bqd0 += w * qd[0]; bqd1 += w * qd[1]; bqd2 += w * qd[2]; bqd3 += w * qd[3];
        }

        const len = Math.sqrt(bqr0*bqr0 + bqr1*bqr1 + bqr2*bqr2 + bqr3*bqr3);
        if (len < 1e-8) {
          px = rx; py = ry; pz = rz;
        } else {
          const inv = 1.0 / len;
          const nqr = [bqr0*inv, bqr1*inv, bqr2*inv, bqr3*inv];
          const nqd = [bqd0*inv, bqd1*inv, bqd2*inv, bqd3*inv];
          const p = dq_apply(nqr, nqd, rx, ry, rz);
          px = p[0]; py = p[1]; pz = p[2];
        }
      } else {
        // --- LBS blend (with hybrid DQS for arm-blended verts) ---
        px = 0; py = 0; pz = 0;
        // Hybrid DQS for arm-blended verts DISABLED: mixing DQS arm verts with LBS
        // torso verts at the shoulder boundary creates seam gaps. The saved good version
        // (2-14) used pure LBS for all non-DQS-mode blending.
        const hasArmWeight = false; // disabled

        if (false) {
          // Hybrid DQS for arm-blended verts
          let bqr0=0, bqr1=0, bqr2=0, bqr3=0;
          let bqd0=0, bqd1=0, bqd2=0, bqd3=0;
          let firstQr = null;
          for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
            const rid = entries[e];
            const w = entries[e+1];
            const dq = regionDQs[rid];
            if (!dq) continue;
            let qr = dq.qr, qd = dq.qd;
            if (!firstQr) { firstQr = qr; }
            else {
              const dot = firstQr[0]*qr[0]+firstQr[1]*qr[1]+firstQr[2]*qr[2]+firstQr[3]*qr[3];
              if (dot < 0) { qr=[-qr[0],-qr[1],-qr[2],-qr[3]]; qd=[-qd[0],-qd[1],-qd[2],-qd[3]]; }
            }
            bqr0+=w*qr[0]; bqr1+=w*qr[1]; bqr2+=w*qr[2]; bqr3+=w*qr[3];
            bqd0+=w*qd[0]; bqd1+=w*qd[1]; bqd2+=w*qd[2]; bqd3+=w*qd[3];
          }
          const len = Math.sqrt(bqr0*bqr0+bqr1*bqr1+bqr2*bqr2+bqr3*bqr3);
          if (len < 1e-8) { px=rx; py=ry; pz=rz; }
          else {
            const inv = 1.0/len;
            const p = dq_apply([bqr0*inv,bqr1*inv,bqr2*inv,bqr3*inv],[bqd0*inv,bqd1*inv,bqd2*inv,bqd3*inv],rx,ry,rz);
            px=p[0]; py=p[1]; pz=p[2];
          }
        } else {

        for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
          const rid = entries[e];
          const w   = entries[e + 1];
          if (w > dominantW) { dominantRid = rid; dominantW = w; }
          const tf  = transforms[rid];
          if (!tf) continue;
          // Twist gradient: blend from no-twist at hip to full twist at knee.
          // Acts as virtual twist bone for thighs (1/2).
          // Eliminates LBS candy-wrapper artifacts from bone-axis twist.
          if (tf.R_noTwist && (rid === 1 || rid === 2)) {
            const piv = tf._hip;
            const d = tf._thighDir;
            const tLen = tf._thighLen;
            const tp = ((rx-piv[0])*d[0] + (ry-piv[1])*d[1] + (rz-piv[2])*d[2]) / tLen;
            const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tp));
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
            const Rn = tf.R_noTwist, tn = tf.t_noTwist;
            const fx = Rn[0]*rx+Rn[1]*ry+Rn[2]*rz+tn[0], fy = Rn[3]*rx+Rn[4]*ry+Rn[5]*rz+tn[1], fz = Rn[6]*rx+Rn[7]*ry+Rn[8]*rz+tn[2];
            const tx2 = R[0]*rx+R[1]*ry+R[2]*rz+t[0], ty2 = R[3]*rx+R[4]*ry+R[5]*rz+t[1], tz2 = R[6]*rx+R[7]*ry+R[8]*rz+t[2];
            px += w * (fx + sf * (tx2 - fx));
            py += w * (fy + sf * (ty2 - fy));
            pz += w * (fz + sf * (tz2 - fz));
          } else if (tf.R_noBend && (rid === 9 || rid === 10)) {
            // Bend gradient: blend from upper arm rotation at elbow (zero seam) to
            // full forearm rotation at wrist. Same pattern as thigh twist gradient.
            const piv = tf._elbowRest;
            const d = tf._forearmDir;
            const fLen = tf._forearmLen;
            const tp = ((rx-piv[0])*d[0] + (ry-piv[1])*d[1] + (rz-piv[2])*d[2]) / fLen;
            const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tp));
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep: 0 at elbow, 1 at wrist
            const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
            const Rn = tf.R_noBend, tn = tf.t_noBend;
            const fx = Rn[0]*rx+Rn[1]*ry+Rn[2]*rz+tn[0], fy = Rn[3]*rx+Rn[4]*ry+Rn[5]*rz+tn[1], fz = Rn[6]*rx+Rn[7]*ry+Rn[8]*rz+tn[2];
            const tx2 = R[0]*rx+R[1]*ry+R[2]*rz+t[0], ty2 = R[3]*rx+R[4]*ry+R[5]*rz+t[1], tz2 = R[6]*rx+R[7]*ry+R[8]*rz+t[2];
            px += w * (fx + sf * (tx2 - fx));
            py += w * (fy + sf * (ty2 - fy));
            pz += w * (fz + sf * (tz2 - fz));
          } else if (spineInterp && (rid === 0 || rid === 14)) {
            // Spine interpolation: height-based blend between pelvis and torso transforms.
            // Gives smooth non-rigid spine bending instead of one rigid average.
            const spT = clamp((ry - spineInterp.pelvisY) * spineInterp.invHeight, 0, 1);
            // Map t to band index pair for lerp between pre-computed bands
            const bandF = spT * 4; // 0..4
            const bandLo = Math.min(3, Math.floor(bandF));
            const bandHi = bandLo + 1;
            const bandMix = bandF - bandLo;
            const bLo = spineInterp.bands[bandLo], bHi = spineInterp.bands[bandHi];
            // Lerp between adjacent band transforms
            const RL = bLo.R, RH = bHi.R, tL = bLo.t, tH = bHi.t;
            const m = bandMix, om = 1 - m;
            // Interpolated rotation applied to rest position + interpolated translation
            const r0 = om*RL[0]+m*RH[0], r1 = om*RL[1]+m*RH[1], r2 = om*RL[2]+m*RH[2];
            const r3 = om*RL[3]+m*RH[3], r4 = om*RL[4]+m*RH[4], r5 = om*RL[5]+m*RH[5];
            const r6 = om*RL[6]+m*RH[6], r7 = om*RL[7]+m*RH[7], r8 = om*RL[8]+m*RH[8];
            const itx = om*tL[0]+m*tH[0], ity = om*tL[1]+m*tH[1], itz = om*tL[2]+m*tH[2];
            px += w * (r0*rx + r1*ry + r2*rz + itx);
            py += w * (r3*rx + r4*ry + r5*rz + ity);
            pz += w * (r6*rx + r7*ry + r8*rz + itz);
          } else {
            const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
            px += w * (R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + t[0]);
            py += w * (R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + t[1]);
            pz += w * (R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + t[2]);
          }
        }
        } // end else (non-hard-arm LBS)
      }

      // Post-blend thigh pull: boost thigh following for thigh-zone verts.
      // Two components: axial (along thigh bone) + medial (inner thigh boost).
      // Outer thigh: pull = 0 at hip (smooth joint), ramps along thigh.
      // Inner thigh: immediate pull even at hip (no visible joint seam between legs).
      // Also applies to torso/pelvis-dominant verts in the inner thigh zone.
      if (!hardRegions) {
        // Determine which thigh to pull toward
        let pullRid = 0;
        if (dominantRid === 1 || dominantRid === 2) pullRid = dominantRid;
        else if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip) {
          // For torso/pelvis-dominant verts: pick nearest thigh by X position
          const dL = Math.abs(rx - P.l_hip[0]), dR = Math.abs(rx - P.r_hip[0]);
          pullRid = dL < dR ? 1 : 2;
        }
        const tf = pullRid > 0 ? transforms[pullRid] : null;
        if (tf && tf._hip) {
          const hip = tf._hip, d = tf._thighDir, tLen = tf._thighLen;
          const proj = ((rx-hip[0])*d[0] + (ry-hip[1])*d[1] + (rz-hip[2])*d[2]) / tLen;
          // Only process verts in the thigh zone
          if (proj > -0.15 && proj < 1.0) {
            // Medial factor: how "inner thigh" is this vert (0=outer, 1=midline)
            const isL = (pullRid === 1);
            const medial = isL ? (rx - hip[0]) : (hip[0] - rx);
            const hipHalfSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
            const medialFrac = clamp(medial / Math.max(hipHalfSpread, 0.01), 0, 1);

            // Compute thigh-only position (with twist gradient if applicable)
            let tpx, tpy, tpz;
            if (tf.R_noTwist) {
              const tp = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, proj));
              const sf = tp * tp * (3 - 2 * tp);
              const R = tf.R, t = tf.t, Rn = tf.R_noTwist, tn = tf.t_noTwist;
              const fx = Rn[0]*rx+Rn[1]*ry+Rn[2]*rz+tn[0];
              const fy = Rn[3]*rx+Rn[4]*ry+Rn[5]*rz+tn[1];
              const fz = Rn[6]*rx+Rn[7]*ry+Rn[8]*rz+tn[2];
              const tx2 = R[0]*rx+R[1]*ry+R[2]*rz+t[0];
              const ty2 = R[3]*rx+R[4]*ry+R[5]*rz+t[1];
              const tz2 = R[6]*rx+R[7]*ry+R[8]*rz+t[2];
              tpx = fx + sf * (tx2 - fx);
              tpy = fy + sf * (ty2 - fy);
              tpz = fz + sf * (tz2 - fz);
            } else {
              const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
              tpx = R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + t[0];
              tpy = R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + t[1];
              tpz = R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + t[2];
            }

            // Pull = medial boost (inner thigh base) + axial ramp (along thigh)
            // Taper near the knee — but extend far enough that thigh verts near
            // the knee still get strong pull. Reaches zero at proj=1.05 (just past
            // the actual knee joint), ensuring the entire thigh region follows the
            // thigh transform. Only thigh-dominant verts get this pull, so shin
            // verts are unaffected.
            const kneeTaper = proj < 0.6 ? 1.0 : clamp((1.05 - proj) / 0.45, 0, 1);
            const medialBoost = medialFrac * 0.7 * kneeTaper;
            const axialPull = clamp(proj * 6, 0, 0.95) * kneeTaper;
            let pull = clamp(medialBoost + axialPull, 0, 0.95);

            // CRITICAL: reduce pull for verts in the cross-thigh blend zone.
            // If a vert has weight from both thighs (e.g. 50/50 at midline),
            // the LBS/DQS blend already places it correctly between both legs.
            // Pulling it toward one thigh would rip it away from its neighbor
            // on the opposite thigh, creating the crotch strand.
            // dominance: 1.0 when 100% one thigh, 0.0 when 50/50 split.
            {
              const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
              let ltw = 0, rtw = 0;
              for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
                if (entries[e] === 1) ltw = entries[e + 1];
                else if (entries[e] === 2) rtw = entries[e + 1];
              }
              const totTh = ltw + rtw;
              const dominance = totTh > 0.01 ? Math.abs(ltw - rtw) / totTh : 1.0;
              pull *= dominance;
            }

            // For torso/pelvis-dominant verts: only apply if inner thigh zone
            const isThighDom = (dominantRid === 1 || dominantRid === 2);
            if (isThighDom || (medialFrac > 0.2 && proj > -0.05 && proj < 0.6)) {
              if (pull > 0.001) {
                px += pull * (tpx - px);
                py += pull * (tpy - py);
                pz += pull * (tpz - pz);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Lerp with alpha stiffness (0 = rest, 1 = fully deformed)
      pos[i*3]   = rx + alpha * (px - rx);
      pos[i*3+1] = ry + alpha * (py - ry);
      pos[i*3+2] = rz + alpha * (pz - rz);

      // Set debug color from dominant region
      if (debugRegions && colors) {
        const c = REGION_COLORS[dominantRid] || [0.5, 0.5, 0.5];
        colors[i*3]   = c[0];
        colors[i*3+1] = c[1];
        colors[i*3+2] = c[2];
      }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // POST-DEFORM BOUNDARY SMOOTHING DISABLED: Not in saved good version (2-14).
    // 3 iterations at 50% on boundary + 25% on fringe displaces verts from
    // their bone-driven positions. The root cause of seam gaps should be fixed
    // at the transform level, not papered over with smoothing.
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    if (false && !hardRegions && meshAdjacency && meshVertRegions) {
      const { adjList, adjStart, adjCount: ac } = meshAdjacency;

      // Precompute which verts are in or adjacent to region boundaries.
      // Includes the 1-ring neighborhood of boundary verts to prevent
      // secondary seams between smoothed boundary verts and hard neighbors.
      if (!window._smoothZoneVerts) {
        const isBoundary = new Uint8Array(nMesh);
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
          if (!entries) continue;
          let nSig = 0;
          for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
            if (entries[e + 1] > 0.05) nSig++;
          }
          if (nSig >= 2) isBoundary[i] = 2; // core boundary
        }
        // Expand to 1-ring neighbors (mark as 1 = fringe)
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (isBoundary[i] !== 2) continue;
          const nc = ac[i], start = adjStart[i];
          for (let j = 0; j < nc; j++) {
            const n = adjList[start + j];
            if (isBoundary[n] === 0) isBoundary[n] = 1;
          }
        }
        const bv = [];
        const smoothWeight = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (isBoundary[i] > 0) {
            bv.push(i);
            smoothWeight.push(isBoundary[i] === 2 ? 0.5 : 0.25); // core=strong, fringe=gentle
          }
        }
        window._smoothZoneVerts = new Uint32Array(bv);
        window._smoothZoneWeights = new Float32Array(smoothWeight);
        console.log('[BOUNDARY SMOOTH] ' + bv.length + ' smooth-zone verts (' +
          bv.filter((_,i) => smoothWeight[i] > 0.3).length + ' core + ' +
          bv.filter((_,i) => smoothWeight[i] <= 0.3).length + ' fringe) of ' + nMesh + ' total');
      }
      const szverts = window._smoothZoneVerts;
      const szweights = window._smoothZoneWeights;

      if (!window._smoothBuf || window._smoothBuf.length !== nMesh * 3) {
        window._smoothBuf = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
      }
      const sbuf = window._smoothBuf;
      for (let iter = 0; iter < 3; iter++) {
        sbuf.set(pos);
        for (let bi = 0; bi < szverts.length; bi++) {
          const v = szverts[bi];
          const v3 = v * 3;
          const nc = ac[v];
          if (nc < 2) continue;
          let avgX = 0, avgY = 0, avgZ = 0;
          const start = adjStart[v];
          for (let j = 0; j < nc; j++) {
            const n = adjList[start + j];
            const n3 = n * 3;
            avgX += sbuf[n3]; avgY += sbuf[n3 + 1]; avgZ += sbuf[n3 + 2];
          }
          avgX /= nc; avgY /= nc; avgZ /= nc;
          const w = szweights[bi];
          pos[v3]     += w * (avgX - pos[v3]);
          pos[v3 + 1] += w * (avgY - pos[v3 + 1]);
          pos[v3 + 2] += w * (avgZ - pos[v3 + 2]);
        }
      }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // ONE-SHOT INNER-THIGH DIAGNOSTIC: find what weights inner-thigh
    // verts near the knee have and why they might not follow the thigh
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    if (!window._innerThighDiagDone && transforms[1] && P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_hip && transforms[1]._hip) {
      window._innerThighDiagDone = true;
      const hip = P.l_hip, knee = P.l_knee;
      const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
      const d = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
      const hipHalfSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
      const R1 = transforms[1].R, t1 = transforms[1].t;
      console.log('═══ INNER-THIGH NEAR KNEE DIAGNOSTIC (left leg) ═══');
      console.log('  hip=' + hip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + ' knee=' + knee.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
      console.log('  thighLen=' + thighLen.toFixed(4) + ' hipHalfSpread=' + hipHalfSpread.toFixed(4));

      // Categorize all left-leg thigh verts by zone
      const zones = { innerLow: [], outerLow: [], innerMid: [], outerMid: [] };
      let totalThighVerts = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
        let lThighW = 0, rThighW = 0, shinW = 0, torsoW = 0, pelvisW = 0, otherW = 0;
        const allWeights = {};
        for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
          const rid = entries[e], w = entries[e+1];
          allWeights[rid] = w;
          if (rid === 1) lThighW = w;
          else if (rid === 2) rThighW = w;
          else if (rid === 3) shinW = w;
          else if (rid === 0) torsoW = w;
          else if (rid === 14) pelvisW = w;
          else otherW += w;
        }
        if (lThighW < 0.01 && shinW < 0.01) continue; // not left leg
        totalThighVerts++;

        const rx = meshRestPos[i*3], ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
        const tp = (rx-hip[0])*d[0] + (ry-hip[1])*d[1] + (rz-hip[2])*d[2];
        const normProj = tp / thighLen;
        // Medial factor: positive = toward midline (inner), negative = outer
        const medial = rx - hip[0]; // positive X = toward body midline for left leg
        const medialFrac = medial / Math.max(hipHalfSpread, 0.01);

        // Compute expected thigh position (with twist gradient if applicable)
        let tx, ty, tz;
        if (transforms[1].R_noTwist) {
          const Rn = transforms[1].R_noTwist, tn = transforms[1].t_noTwist;
          const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, normProj));
          const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
          const fx = Rn[0]*rx+Rn[1]*ry+Rn[2]*rz+tn[0];
          const fy = Rn[3]*rx+Rn[4]*ry+Rn[5]*rz+tn[1];
          const fz = Rn[6]*rx+Rn[7]*ry+Rn[8]*rz+tn[2];
          const gx = R1[0]*rx+R1[1]*ry+R1[2]*rz+t1[0];
          const gy = R1[3]*rx+R1[4]*ry+R1[5]*rz+t1[1];
          const gz = R1[6]*rx+R1[7]*ry+R1[8]*rz+t1[2];
          tx = fx + sf * (gx - fx); ty = fy + sf * (gy - fy); tz = fz + sf * (gz - fz);
        } else {
          tx = R1[0]*rx+R1[1]*ry+R1[2]*rz+t1[0];
          ty = R1[3]*rx+R1[4]*ry+R1[5]*rz+t1[1];
          tz = R1[6]*rx+R1[7]*ry+R1[8]*rz+t1[2];
        }
        const cx = pos[i*3], cy = pos[i*3+1], cz = pos[i*3+2];
        const err = Math.sqrt((tx-cx)**2+(ty-cy)**2+(tz-cz)**2);

        const info = {
          vi: i, proj: normProj, medialFrac: medialFrac,
          lThighW, rThighW, shinW, torsoW, pelvisW, otherW,
          allWeights, err,
          rest: [rx,ry,rz], thighPos: [tx,ty,tz], curPos: [cx,cy,cz]
        };

        const isInner = medialFrac > 0.15;
        const isLow = normProj > 0.50 && normProj < 1.15;
        const isMid = normProj > 0.25 && normProj <= 0.50;

        if (isInner && isLow) zones.innerLow.push(info);
        else if (!isInner && isLow) zones.outerLow.push(info);
        else if (isInner && isMid) zones.innerMid.push(info);
        else if (!isInner && isMid) zones.outerMid.push(info);
      }

      console.log('  Total left-leg thigh/shin verts: ' + totalThighVerts);
      for (const [zoneName, verts] of Object.entries(zones)) {
        if (verts.length === 0) continue;
        // Compute stats for this zone
        let avgThighW = 0, avgNonThighW = 0, avgErr = 0;
        let maxErr = 0, maxErrVi = -1;
        let hasRThigh = 0, hasTorso = 0, hasPelvis = 0, hasShin = 0;
        for (const v of verts) {
          avgThighW += v.lThighW;
          avgNonThighW += (v.rThighW + v.torsoW + v.pelvisW + v.shinW + v.otherW);
          avgErr += v.err;
          if (v.err > maxErr) { maxErr = v.err; maxErrVi = v.vi; }
          if (v.rThighW > 0.01) hasRThigh++;
          if (v.torsoW > 0.01) hasTorso++;
          if (v.pelvisW > 0.01) hasPelvis++;
          if (v.shinW > 0.01) hasShin++;
        }
        const n = verts.length;
        avgThighW /= n; avgNonThighW /= n; avgErr /= n;
        console.log('  --- ' + zoneName + ' (' + n + ' verts) ---');
        console.log('    avgThighW=' + avgThighW.toFixed(3) + ' avgNonThighW=' + avgNonThighW.toFixed(3));
        console.log('    avgErr(cur vs thigh-only)=' + avgErr.toFixed(5) + ' maxErr=' + maxErr.toFixed(5) + ' at v' + maxErrVi);
        console.log('    hasRThigh=' + hasRThigh + ' hasTorso=' + hasTorso + ' hasPelvis=' + hasPelvis + ' hasShin=' + hasShin);

        // Show 8 worst-error verts from this zone
        verts.sort((a,b) => b.err - a.err);
        const show = Math.min(8, verts.length);
        for (let s = 0; s < show; s++) {
          const v = verts[s];
          const wStr = Object.entries(v.allWeights).map(([r,w]) => 'r'+r+'='+w.toFixed(3)).join(' ');
          console.log('    v' + v.vi + ' proj=' + v.proj.toFixed(2) + ' medial=' + v.medialFrac.toFixed(2) +
            ' err=' + v.err.toFixed(5) + ' | ' + wStr +
            ' | rest=[' + v.rest.map(x=>x.toFixed(4)) + ']' +
            ' cur=[' + v.curPos.map(x=>x.toFixed(4)) + ']' +
            ' thighOnly=[' + v.thighPos.map(x=>x.toFixed(4)) + ']');
        }
      }

      // Also show what the thigh transform IS doing
      console.log('  --- Thigh transform check ---');
      const tf1 = transforms[1];
      console.log('  R=' + tf1.R.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
      console.log('  t=' + tf1.t.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
      if (tf1.R_noTwist) console.log('  R_noTwist=' + tf1.R_noTwist.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
      if (tf1.t_noTwist) console.log('  t_noTwist=' + tf1.t_noTwist.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
    }

    // POST-DQS CROTCH CORRECTION + PBD: DISABLED
    // These post-processing passes were overriding the DQS weight-based
    // deformation — pulling 2559 verts toward pelvis and yanking 12k edges
    // every frame, causing back-of-thigh mesh distortion. The pelvis weight
    // injection now handles crotch deformation through DQS directly.
    // [crotch correction and PBD removed — DQS with pelvis weights is the output]

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // ONE-SHOT STRETCH DIAGNOSTIC: find WHY crotch verts stretch
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    if (!window._stretchDiagDone && transforms[0] && (transforms[1] || transforms[2]) && P.pelvis) {
      window._stretchDiagDone = true;
      const R0 = transforms[0].R, t0 = transforms[0].t;
      const midX = P.pelvis[0]; // body midline X

      // Collect inner thigh verts (near midline, between hip and mid-thigh height)
      const diagVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const rrx = meshRestPos[i*3], rry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rrz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
        const entries2 = meshVertRegions[i];
        let tw = 0, tRid = 0, toW = 0, pelW = 0;
        for (let e = 0; e < entries2.length; e += 2) {
          if (entries2[e] === 0) toW = entries2[e+1];
          if (entries2[e] === 14) pelW = entries2[e+1];
          if (entries2[e] === 1 || entries2[e] === 2) { tw = entries2[e+1]; tRid = entries2[e]; }
        }
        if (tw < 0.01) continue; // skip verts with no thigh weight
        // Inner thigh: close to midline (within 3cm of center X)
        if (Math.abs(rrx - midX) > 0.03) continue;
        // Between hip and mid-thigh height
        const hipY = P.l_hip ? P.l_hip[1] : P.r_hip[1];
        const kneeY = P.l_knee ? P.l_knee[1] : P.r_knee[1];
        const midThighY = (hipY + kneeY) / 2;
        if (rry > hipY + 0.02 || rry < midThighY) continue;

        // Compute individual transform results
        const torsoX = R0[0]*rrx + R0[1]*rry + R0[2]*rrz + t0[0];
        const torsoY = R0[3]*rrx + R0[4]*rry + R0[5]*rrz + t0[1];
        const torsoZ = R0[6]*rrx + R0[7]*rry + R0[8]*rrz + t0[2];
        let thighX = rrx, thighY = rry, thighZ = rrz;
        if (transforms[tRid]) {
          const Rt = transforms[tRid].R, tt = transforms[tRid].t;
          thighX = Rt[0]*rrx + Rt[1]*rry + Rt[2]*rrz + tt[0];
          thighY = Rt[3]*rrx + Rt[4]*rry + Rt[5]*rrz + tt[1];
          thighZ = Rt[6]*rrx + Rt[7]*rry + Rt[8]*rrz + tt[2];
        }
        // DQS/LBS result (already in pos array)
        const fx = pos[i*3], fy = pos[i*3+1], fz = pos[i*3+2];

        diagVerts.push({
          i, rest: [rrx, rry, rrz],
          thighW: tw, torsoW: toW, pelvisW: pelW, thighRid: tRid,
          torsoPos: [torsoX, torsoY, torsoZ],
          thighPos: [thighX, thighY, thighZ],
          finalPos: [fx, fy, fz],
          // Distance from torso and thigh individual results
          distFromTorso: Math.sqrt((fx-torsoX)**2+(fy-torsoY)**2+(fz-torsoZ)**2),
          distFromThigh: Math.sqrt((fx-thighX)**2+(fy-thighY)**2+(fz-thighZ)**2)
        });
      }

      // Sort by thigh weight descending to see the worst offenders
      diagVerts.sort((a, b) => b.thighW - a.thighW);

      console.log('═══ CROTCH STRETCH DIAGNOSTIC (bone-proximity weight suppression) ═══');
      console.log('Inner thigh verts found (near midline, hip-to-mid-thigh): ' + diagVerts.length);

      // Show the top 15 highest thigh-weight crotch verts
      const show = diagVerts.slice(0, 15);
      for (const v of show) {
        // Compute bone-proximity ratio for this vert
        let bpRatio = -1;
        if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
          const dL = pointToSegmentDist(v.rest[0], v.rest[1], v.rest[2], P.l_hip, P.l_knee);
          const dR = pointToSegmentDist(v.rest[0], v.rest[1], v.rest[2], P.r_hip, P.r_knee);
          const dMin = Math.min(dL, dR), dMax = Math.max(dL, dR);
          bpRatio = dMax > 1e-6 ? dMin / dMax : 1.0;
        }
        console.log('  v' + v.i +
          ' thighW=' + v.thighW.toFixed(3) + ' torsoW=' + v.torsoW.toFixed(3) +
          ' pelvisW=' + v.pelvisW.toFixed(3) +
          ' rid=' + v.thighRid + ' bpRatio=' + bpRatio.toFixed(3) +
          ' rest=[' + v.rest.map(x=>x.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']' +
          ' final=[' + v.finalPos.map(x=>x.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']' +
          ' dTorso=' + v.distFromTorso.toFixed(4) +
          ' dThigh=' + v.distFromThigh.toFixed(4));
      }

      // Summary: weight distribution of crotch verts
      const w100 = diagVerts.filter(v => v.thighW > 0.99).length;
      const w80 = diagVerts.filter(v => v.thighW > 0.80 && v.thighW <= 0.99).length;
      const w60 = diagVerts.filter(v => v.thighW > 0.60 && v.thighW <= 0.80).length;
      const wLow = diagVerts.filter(v => v.thighW <= 0.60).length;
      const hasPelvis = diagVerts.filter(v => v.pelvisW > 0.01).length;
      console.log('  Weight distribution: >99%=' + w100 + ' 80-99%=' + w80 + ' 60-80%=' + w60 + ' <60%=' + wLow + ' | has pelvis(14): ' + hasPelvis);

      // Find worst stretch: max distance between final positions of adjacent crotch verts
      // that are close in rest pose but far in deformed pose
      let worstStretch = 0, worstPair = null;
      for (let a = 0; a < diagVerts.length; a++) {
        for (let b = a + 1; b < Math.min(diagVerts.length, a + 50); b++) {
          const restDist = Math.sqrt(
            (diagVerts[a].rest[0]-diagVerts[b].rest[0])**2 +
            (diagVerts[a].rest[1]-diagVerts[b].rest[1])**2 +
            (diagVerts[a].rest[2]-diagVerts[b].rest[2])**2);
          if (restDist > 0.02 || restDist < 0.001) continue; // skip far and degenerate pairs
          const defDist = Math.sqrt(
            (diagVerts[a].finalPos[0]-diagVerts[b].finalPos[0])**2 +
            (diagVerts[a].finalPos[1]-diagVerts[b].finalPos[1])**2 +
            (diagVerts[a].finalPos[2]-diagVerts[b].finalPos[2])**2);
          const stretch = defDist / Math.max(restDist, 0.001);
          if (stretch > worstStretch) {
            worstStretch = stretch;
            worstPair = [diagVerts[a], diagVerts[b], restDist, defDist];
          }
        }
      }
      if (worstPair) {
        console.log('  WORST STRETCH: ' + worstStretch.toFixed(1) + 'x' +
          ' (rest=' + worstPair[2].toFixed(4) + ' def=' + worstPair[3].toFixed(4) + ')');
        console.log('    vertA: v' + worstPair[0].i +
          ' thighW=' + worstPair[0].thighW.toFixed(3) +
          ' torsoW=' + worstPair[0].torsoW.toFixed(3) +
          ' rid=' + worstPair[0].thighRid);
        console.log('    vertB: v' + worstPair[1].i +
          ' thighW=' + worstPair[1].thighW.toFixed(3) +
          ' torsoW=' + worstPair[1].torsoW.toFixed(3) +
          ' rid=' + worstPair[1].thighRid);
      }
      console.log('═══ END STRETCH DIAGNOSTIC ═══');
    }

    // ONE-SHOT: Boundary seam gap diagnostic — measures transform discontinuity
    // at each region boundary. Waits until position-based driving is active
    // (armState.positionBased) so we measure the actual animation, not uninitialized state.
    if (!window._seamGapDiagDone && transforms && meshVertRegions && meshRestPos &&
        armState) {
      window._seamGapDiagDone = true;
      console.log('═══ BOUNDARY SEAM GAP DIAGNOSTIC ═══');
      const BOUNDARY_PAIRS = [
        [0, 7,  'torso↔l_uarm (L shoulder)'],
        [0, 8,  'torso↔r_uarm (R shoulder)'],
        [0, 1,  'torso↔l_thigh'],
        [0, 2,  'torso↔r_thigh'],
        [7, 9,  'l_uarm↔l_farm (L elbow)'],
        [8, 10, 'r_uarm↔r_farm (R elbow)'],
        [1, 3,  'l_thigh↔l_shin (L knee)'],
        [2, 4,  'r_thigh↔r_shin (R knee)'],
        [0, 11, 'torso↔head (neck)'],
        [14, 0, 'pelvis↔torso']
      ];
      for (const [ridA, ridB, label] of BOUNDARY_PAIRS) {
        const tfA = transforms[ridA], tfB = transforms[ridB];
        if (!tfA || !tfB) continue;
        let count = 0, totalGap = 0, maxGap = 0, maxGapVert = -1;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
          if (!entries || !entries.length) continue;
          let wA = 0, wB = 0;
          for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
            if (entries[e] === ridA) wA = entries[e+1];
            else if (entries[e] === ridB) wB = entries[e+1];
          }
          if (wA > 0.05 && wB > 0.05) {
            const rx = meshRestPos[i*3], ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
            const pA = mat3_mulVec3(tfA.R, rx, ry, rz);
            const pB = mat3_mulVec3(tfB.R, rx, ry, rz);
            const ax = pA[0]+tfA.t[0], ay = pA[1]+tfA.t[1], az = pA[2]+tfA.t[2];
            const bx = pB[0]+tfB.t[0], by = pB[1]+tfB.t[1], bz = pB[2]+tfB.t[2];
            const gap = Math.sqrt((ax-bx)*(ax-bx)+(ay-by)*(ay-by)+(az-bz)*(az-bz));
            totalGap += gap;
            if (gap > maxGap) { maxGap = gap; maxGapVert = i; }
            count++;
          }
        }
        if (count > 0) {
          const avgGap = totalGap / count * 1000;
          const maxGapMm = maxGap * 1000;
          // Shoulders have inherently larger transform gaps (arm rotates differently from torso)
          const isShoulder = label.includes('shoulder');
          const status = isShoulder
            ? (maxGapMm < 50 ? 'GOOD' : maxGapMm < 120 ? 'OK' : maxGapMm < 200 ? 'WARN' : 'BAD')
            : (maxGapMm < 10 ? 'GOOD' : maxGapMm < 30 ? 'OK' : maxGapMm < 80 ? 'WARN' : 'BAD');
          console.log('  ' + label + ': ' + count + ' boundary verts, avg=' +
            avgGap.toFixed(1) + 'mm, max=' + maxGapMm.toFixed(1) + 'mm (v' + maxGapVert + ') ' + status);
        }
      }
      console.log('═══ END SEAM GAP DIAGNOSTIC ═══');
    }

    // Adaptive arm twist DISABLED: the saved good version (2-14) used constant twist scale.
    // Reducing twist based on arm swing changes proportions and can introduce mismatches.
    _armTwistScale.l = 1.0;
    _armTwistScale.r = 1.0;

    // Delta Mush correction (strain-weighted: only fixes verts with actual deformation problems)
    applyDeltaMush(pos);

    // Collapse cross-boundary crotch edges to eliminate strand between thighs
    collapseStretchedEdges(pos);

    // Global safety-net edge collapse DISABLED: 4 iterations over ALL mesh edges
    // displaces vertices from their bone-driven positions, causing cumulative drift.
    // The targeted cross-boundary collapse (collapseStretchedEdges) is still active.
    // The saved good version (2-14) did not have global collapse.

    // Global Laplacian spatial smoothing DISABLED: applying 8% smoothing across ALL
    // vertices every frame degrades mesh detail and can introduce artifacts when stacked
    // with other post-processing. The saved good version (2-14) did not have this.

    // Triangle inversion correction DISABLED: aggressive 45% factor with 3 iterations
    // was pushing vertices in wrong directions, compounding with other post-processing layers.
    // The saved good version (2-14) did not have this.
    if (false && meshAdjacency && meshGeo && meshGeo.index && meshRestPos) {
      const triIdx = meshGeo.index.array;
      const nTri = triIdx.length / 3;
      const { adjList: aL, adjStart: aS, adjCount: aC } = meshAdjacency;

      const INV_ITERS = 3;
      const INV_FACTOR = 0.45;
      for (let iter = 0; iter < INV_ITERS; iter++) {
        const invFlag = new Uint8Array(nMesh);
        let invCount = 0;
        for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
          const a = triIdx[t*3], b = triIdx[t*3+1], c = triIdx[t*3+2];
          const a3=a*3, b3=b*3, c3=c*3;
          const e1x=pos[b3]-pos[a3], e1y=pos[b3+1]-pos[a3+1], e1z=pos[b3+2]-pos[a3+2];
          const e2x=pos[c3]-pos[a3], e2y=pos[c3+1]-pos[a3+1], e2z=pos[c3+2]-pos[a3+2];
          const dnx=e1y*e2z-e1z*e2y, dny=e1z*e2x-e1x*e2z, dnz=e1x*e2y-e1y*e2x;
          const r1x=meshRestPos[b3]-meshRestPos[a3], r1y=meshRestPos[b3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r1z=meshRestPos[b3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
          const r2x=meshRestPos[c3]-meshRestPos[a3], r2y=meshRestPos[c3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r2z=meshRestPos[c3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
          const rnx=r1y*r2z-r1z*r2y, rny=r1z*r2x-r1x*r2z, rnz=r1x*r2y-r1y*r2x;
          if (dnx*rnx + dny*rny + dnz*rnz < 0) {
            invFlag[a] = 1; invFlag[b] = 1; invFlag[c] = 1;
            invCount++;
          }
        }
        if (invCount === 0) break;
        for (let v = 0; v < nMesh; v++) {
          if (!invFlag[v]) continue;
          const nc = aC[v];
          if (nc < 2) continue;
          const v3 = v * 3;
          let sx = 0, sy = 0, sz = 0;
          const start = aS[v];
          for (let j = 0; j < nc; j++) {
            const nb = aL[start + j];
            sx += pos[nb*3]; sy += pos[nb*3+1]; sz += pos[nb*3+2];
          }
          const invN = 1.0 / nc;
          pos[v3]   += INV_FACTOR * (sx * invN - pos[v3]);
          pos[v3+1] += INV_FACTOR * (sy * invN - pos[v3+1]);
          pos[v3+2] += INV_FACTOR * (sz * invN - pos[v3+2]);
        }
      }
    }

    // Per-vertex jitter damping: acceleration-cancellation approach.
    // Blends toward zero-acceleration position (prev + prevVelocity) to preserve
    // smooth motion while reducing sudden direction changes.
    // Conservative settings: only catch genuine high-frequency noise, not natural motion.
    if (prevMeshPos && prevMeshPos.length === pos.length) {
      if (!_jitterVel) _jitterVel = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
      const ACCEL_THRESH = 0.025;  // 25mm/f^2 — higher threshold to preserve natural motion
      const MAX_DAMP = 0.50;       // max 50% accel cancellation
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const i3 = i * 3;
        const vx = pos[i3] - prevMeshPos[i3];
        const vy = pos[i3+1] - prevMeshPos[i3+1];
        const vz = pos[i3+2] - prevMeshPos[i3+2];
        const ax = vx - _jitterVel[i3];
        const ay = vy - _jitterVel[i3+1];
        const az = vz - _jitterVel[i3+2];
        const accel = Math.sqrt(ax*ax + ay*ay + az*az);
        if (accel > ACCEL_THRESH) {
          const ratio = (accel - ACCEL_THRESH) / accel;
          const d = Math.min(ratio * 0.8, MAX_DAMP);
          // Target: position that would give zero acceleration (constant velocity)
          const tx = prevMeshPos[i3]   + _jitterVel[i3];
          const ty = prevMeshPos[i3+1] + _jitterVel[i3+1];
          const tz = prevMeshPos[i3+2] + _jitterVel[i3+2];
          pos[i3]   = pos[i3]   * (1-d) + tx * d;
          pos[i3+1] = pos[i3+1] * (1-d) + ty * d;
          pos[i3+2] = pos[i3+2] * (1-d) + tz * d;
        }
      }
      // Update velocity AFTER all damping
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh * 3; i++) {
        _jitterVel[i] = pos[i] - prevMeshPos[i];
      }
    }
    if (!prevMeshPos || prevMeshPos.length !== pos.length) {
      prevMeshPos = new Float32Array(pos.length);
    }
    prevMeshPos.set(pos);

    // Ground-plane correction is handled at the cage level in driveCageFromMixamo
    // (lastGroundCorrection). No mesh-level correction needed — it would double-lift.

    // ONE-SHOT: after collapse, scan ALL mesh triangles for worst stretch
    if (!window._postCollapseDiagDone && meshGeo && meshGeo.index && meshVertRegions) {
      window._postCollapseDiagDone = true;
      const triIdx = meshGeo.index.array;
      const nTri = triIdx.length / 3;
      let worstRatio = 0, worstTri = null;
      const stretchedTris = []; // collect all tris with >5x stretch on any edge
      for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
        const a = triIdx[t*3], b = triIdx[t*3+1], c = triIdx[t*3+2];
        const pairs = [[a,b],[b,c],[a,c]];
        for (const [v0,v1] of pairs) {
          // rest length
          const rx = meshRestPos[v1*3]-meshRestPos[v0*3], ry = meshRestPos[v1*3+1]-meshRestPos[v0*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[v1*3+2]-meshRestPos[v0*3+2];
          const rLen = Math.sqrt(rx*rx+ry*ry+rz*rz);
          if (rLen < 1e-8) continue;
          // deformed length (AFTER collapse)
          const dx = pos[v1*3]-pos[v0*3], dy = pos[v1*3+1]-pos[v0*3+1], dz = pos[v1*3+2]-pos[v0*3+2];
          const dLen = Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz);
          const ratio = dLen / rLen;
          if (ratio > 5.0) {
            const getDom = (vi) => { const e = meshVertRegions[vi]; if (!e) return -1; let mw=0,mr=-1; for(let i=0;i<e.length;i+=2){if(e[i+1]>mw){mw=e[i+1];mr=e[i];}} return mr; };
            stretchedTris.push({
              v0, v1, ratio, rLen, dLen,
              r0: getDom(v0), r1: getDom(v1),
              rest0: [meshRestPos[v0*3],meshRestPos[v0*3+1],meshRestPos[v0*3+2]],
              rest1: [meshRestPos[v1*3],meshRestPos[v1*3+1],meshRestPos[v1*3+2]],
              def0: [pos[v0*3],pos[v0*3+1],pos[v0*3+2]],
              def1: [pos[v1*3],pos[v1*3+1],pos[v1*3+2]]
            });
          }
          if (ratio > worstRatio) { worstRatio = ratio; worstTri = {v0,v1,ratio,rLen,dLen}; }
        }
      }
      console.log('═══ POST-COLLAPSE FULL MESH SCAN ═══');
      console.log('  Edges with >5x stretch (AFTER collapse): ' + stretchedTris.length);
      // Sort by ratio descending, show top 20
      stretchedTris.sort((a,b) => b.ratio - a.ratio);
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(20, stretchedTris.length); i++) {
        const s = stretchedTris[i];
        console.log('  v' + s.v0 + '(r' + s.r0 + ') ↔ v' + s.v1 + '(r' + s.r1 + '): ' +
          s.ratio.toFixed(1) + 'x (rest=' + s.rLen.toFixed(4) + ' def=' + s.dLen.toFixed(4) + ')' +
          ' rest0=' + s.rest0.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) + ' rest1=' + s.rest1.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) +
          ' def0=' + s.def0.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) + ' def1=' + s.def1.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)));
      }
      if (worstTri) console.log('  WORST OVERALL: v' + worstTri.v0 + '↔v' + worstTri.v1 + ' ' + worstTri.ratio.toFixed(1) + 'x');
      // Check if collapse actually fired
      console.log('  stretchConstraints edges: ' + (stretchConstraints ? stretchConstraints.nEdges : 'N/A'));
      console.log('  crossBoundaryEdges: ' + (crossBoundaryEdges ? crossBoundaryEdges.nEdges : 'N/A'));
    }

    // Strain visualization (overrides debug region colors when active)
    const showStrain = $('showStrain').checked;
    if (showStrain && colors) {
      computeStrainColors(pos, colors);
    }

    // Bone vs cage comparison heatmap (overrides strain/region colors when active)
    if (showBoneError && colors && retargetedJoints.pelvis) {
      if (!boneRefPos || boneRefPos.length !== nMesh * 3) {
        boneRefPos = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
      }
      if (computeBoneSkinnedPositions(boneRefPos)) {
        computeComparisonColors(pos, boneRefPos, colors);
      }
    }

    // Surface health heatmap: red=bulge, blue=cave-in, green=normal, magenta=inverted triangle
    // Measures non-rigid deformation by subtracting per-region rigid transform
    if (showSurfaceHealth && colors && meshRestNormals && transforms) {
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const i3 = i * 3;
        const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
        let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
        if (entries) { for (let e=0; e<entries.length; e+=2) { if (entries[e+1]>domW) { domRid=entries[e]; domW=entries[e+1]; } } }
        const tf = transforms[domRid];
        if (!tf) { colors[i3]=0.5; colors[i3+1]=0.5; colors[i3+2]=0.5; continue; }
        const rx = meshRestPos[i3], ry = meshRestPos[i3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i3+2];
        const R = tf.R, tt = tf.t;
        const rpx = R[0]*rx+R[1]*ry+R[2]*rz+tt[0];
        const rpy = R[3]*rx+R[4]*ry+R[5]*rz+tt[1];
        const rpz = R[6]*rx+R[7]*ry+R[8]*rz+tt[2];
        const dx = pos[i3]-rpx, dy = pos[i3+1]-rpy, dz = pos[i3+2]-rpz;
        const nx = R[0]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[1]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[2]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        const ny = R[3]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[4]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[5]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        const nz = R[6]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[7]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[8]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        const ndMM = (dx*nx + dy*ny + dz*nz) * 1000;
        let r, g, b;
        if (ndMM > 1) { // bulge: green→yellow→red
          const t = Math.min(ndMM / 20, 1);
          r = t; g = 0.8 * (1 - t * 0.5); b = 0;
        } else if (ndMM < -1) { // cave-in: green→cyan→blue
          const t = Math.min(-ndMM / 20, 1);
          r = 0; g = 0.8 * (1 - t); b = t;
        } else { // normal
          r = 0; g = 0.8; b = 0;
        }
        colors[i3] = r; colors[i3+1] = g; colors[i3+2] = b;
      }
      // Per-frame: detect genuinely inverted triangles and mark in magenta
      if (meshGeo && meshGeo.index) {
        const triIdx = meshGeo.index.array;
        const nTri = triIdx.length / 3;
        for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
          const a = triIdx[t*3], b = triIdx[t*3+1], c = triIdx[t*3+2];
          const a3=a*3, b3=b*3, c3=c*3;
          const e1x=pos[b3]-pos[a3], e1y=pos[b3+1]-pos[a3+1], e1z=pos[b3+2]-pos[a3+2];
          const e2x=pos[c3]-pos[a3], e2y=pos[c3+1]-pos[a3+1], e2z=pos[c3+2]-pos[a3+2];
          const dnx=e1y*e2z-e1z*e2y, dny=e1z*e2x-e1x*e2z, dnz=e1x*e2y-e1y*e2x;
          const r1x=meshRestPos[b3]-meshRestPos[a3], r1y=meshRestPos[b3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r1z=meshRestPos[b3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
          const r2x=meshRestPos[c3]-meshRestPos[a3], r2y=meshRestPos[c3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r2z=meshRestPos[c3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
          const rnx=r1y*r2z-r1z*r2y, rny=r1z*r2x-r1x*r2z, rnz=r1x*r2y-r1y*r2x;
          // Get dominant region transform
          const entries = meshVertRegions[a];
          let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
          if (entries) { for (let e=0; e<entries.length; e+=2) { if (entries[e+1]>domW) { domRid=entries[e]; domW=entries[e+1]; } } }
          const tf = transforms[domRid];
          let dotN;
          if (tf) {
            const R = tf.R;
            const rnx_r = R[0]*rnx+R[1]*rny+R[2]*rnz;
            const rny_r = R[3]*rnx+R[4]*rny+R[5]*rnz;
            const rnz_r = R[6]*rnx+R[7]*rny+R[8]*rnz;
            dotN = dnx*rnx_r + dny*rny_r + dnz*rnz_r;
          } else {
            dotN = dnx*rnx + dny*rny + dnz*rnz;
          }
          if (dotN < 0) {
            colors[a3]=1; colors[a3+1]=0; colors[a3+2]=0.8;
            colors[b3]=1; colors[b3+1]=0; colors[b3+2]=0.8;
            colors[c3]=1; colors[c3+1]=0; colors[c3+2]=0.8;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // ONE-SHOT: Surface health diagnostic — detect inverted triangles (holes) and bulge/cave-in
    // Uses per-region rigid transforms to isolate non-rigid deformation (subtracts body movement)
    if (!window._surfaceHealthDiagDone && meshGeo && meshGeo.index && meshRestNormals && meshVertRegions && transforms) {
      window._surfaceHealthDiagDone = true;
      const triIdx = meshGeo.index.array;
      const nTri = triIdx.length / 3;
      const regionNames = {0:'torso',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
        5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
        11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis'};
      // Per-vertex: compute residual displacement AFTER subtracting rigid transform
      // This isolates non-rigid deformation (blending artifacts, pull, smoothing)
      const normalDisp = new Float32Array(nMesh);
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const i3 = i*3;
        const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
        let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
        if (entries) { for (let e=0; e<entries.length; e+=2) { if (entries[e+1]>domW) { domRid=entries[e]; domW=entries[e+1]; } } }
        const tf = transforms[domRid];
        if (!tf) continue;
        const rx = meshRestPos[i3], ry = meshRestPos[i3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i3+2];
        const R = tf.R, t = tf.t;
        // Where rigid transform places this vertex
        const rpx = R[0]*rx+R[1]*ry+R[2]*rz+t[0];
        const rpy = R[3]*rx+R[4]*ry+R[5]*rz+t[1];
        const rpz = R[6]*rx+R[7]*ry+R[8]*rz+t[2];
        // Residual (actual - rigid)
        const dx = pos[i3]-rpx, dy = pos[i3+1]-rpy, dz = pos[i3+2]-rpz;
        // Rotate rest normal by R to get deformed normal direction
        const nx = R[0]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[1]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[2]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        const ny = R[3]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[4]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[5]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        const nz = R[6]*meshRestNormals[i3]+R[7]*meshRestNormals[i3+1]+R[8]*meshRestNormals[i3+2];
        normalDisp[i] = dx*nx + dy*ny + dz*nz;
      }
      // Per-triangle: check for REAL normal inversion (compare deformed vs rigidly-transformed)
      // Previous approach compared deformed vs REST normal — this produces false positives
      // whenever a region rotates >90° from rest (e.g. 94° elbow bend).
      // Fix: rotate the rest normal by the dominant region's rigid transform first.
      let invertedCount = 0;
      const invertedPerRegion = {};
      const triPerRegion = {};
      const invertedTris = new Uint8Array(nTri); // for visualization
      for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
        const a = triIdx[t*3], b = triIdx[t*3+1], c = triIdx[t*3+2];
        const a3=a*3, b3=b*3, c3=c*3;
        // Deformed face normal
        const e1x=pos[b3]-pos[a3], e1y=pos[b3+1]-pos[a3+1], e1z=pos[b3+2]-pos[a3+2];
        const e2x=pos[c3]-pos[a3], e2y=pos[c3+1]-pos[a3+1], e2z=pos[c3+2]-pos[a3+2];
        const dnx=e1y*e2z-e1z*e2y, dny=e1z*e2x-e1x*e2z, dnz=e1x*e2y-e1y*e2x;
        // Rest face normal
        const r1x=meshRestPos[b3]-meshRestPos[a3], r1y=meshRestPos[b3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r1z=meshRestPos[b3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
        const r2x=meshRestPos[c3]-meshRestPos[a3], r2y=meshRestPos[c3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], r2z=meshRestPos[c3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
        const rnx=r1y*r2z-r1z*r2y, rny=r1z*r2x-r1x*r2z, rnz=r1x*r2y-r1y*r2x;
        // Get dominant region's rigid transform for this triangle
        const entries = meshVertRegions[a];
        let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
        if (entries) { for (let e=0; e<entries.length; e+=2) { if (entries[e+1]>domW) { domRid=entries[e]; domW=entries[e+1]; } } }
        if (!triPerRegion[domRid]) triPerRegion[domRid] = 0;
        triPerRegion[domRid]++;
        // Rotate rest normal by region's rigid transform
        const tf = transforms[domRid];
        let dotN;
        if (tf) {
          const R = tf.R;
          const rnx_r = R[0]*rnx+R[1]*rny+R[2]*rnz;
          const rny_r = R[3]*rnx+R[4]*rny+R[5]*rnz;
          const rnz_r = R[6]*rnx+R[7]*rny+R[8]*rnz;
          dotN = dnx*rnx_r + dny*rny_r + dnz*rnz_r;
        } else {
          dotN = dnx*rnx + dny*rny + dnz*rnz;
        }
        if (dotN < 0) {
          invertedCount++;
          invertedTris[t] = 1;
          if (!invertedPerRegion[domRid]) invertedPerRegion[domRid] = 0;
          invertedPerRegion[domRid]++;
        }
      }
      // Store for surface health visualization
      window._invertedTris = invertedTris;
      window._triIdx = triIdx;
      // Per-region bulge/cave stats (now showing non-rigid deformation only)
      const regionBulge = {};
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
        let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
        if (entries) { for (let e=0; e<entries.length; e+=2) { if (entries[e+1]>domW) { domRid=entries[e]; domW=entries[e+1]; } } }
        if (!regionBulge[domRid]) regionBulge[domRid] = { sumP:0,cntP:0,maxP:0,maxPVert:0,sumN:0,cntN:0,minN:0,minNVert:0,cnt:0 };
        const s = regionBulge[domRid]; s.cnt++;
        const nd = normalDisp[i];
        if (nd > 0) { s.sumP+=nd; s.cntP++; if(nd>s.maxP){s.maxP=nd;s.maxPVert=i;} }
        else { s.sumN+=nd; s.cntN++; if(nd<s.minN){s.minN=nd;s.minNVert=i;} }
      }
      console.log('=== SURFACE HEALTH DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('-- TRIANGLE INVERSIONS (holes/folds) --');
      const invPct = (invertedCount/nTri*100).toFixed(2);
      const invStatus = invertedCount === 0 ? 'GOOD' : invertedCount < nTri*0.001 ? 'OK' : invertedCount < nTri*0.01 ? 'WARN' : 'BAD';
      console.log('  Total inverted: ' + invertedCount + '/' + nTri + ' (' + invPct + '%) ' + invStatus);
      if (invertedCount > 0) {
        for (const rid of Object.keys(invertedPerRegion).sort((a,b)=>invertedPerRegion[b]-invertedPerRegion[a])) {
          const total = triPerRegion[rid] || 1;
          const regPct = invertedPerRegion[rid]/total*100;
          const regStatus = regPct > 10 ? 'CATASTROPHIC' : regPct > 5 ? 'CRITICAL' : regPct > 1 ? 'BAD' : 'WARN';
          console.log('    ' + (regionNames[rid]||'r'+rid) + ': ' + invertedPerRegion[rid] + '/' + total +
            ' (' + regPct.toFixed(1) + '%) ' + regStatus);
        }
      }
      console.log('-- NORMAL DISPLACEMENT (non-rigid: bulge=outward, cave=inward) --');
      for (const rid of Object.keys(regionBulge).sort((a,b)=>+a - +b)) {
        const s = regionBulge[rid];
        const avgB = s.cntP > 0 ? (s.sumP/s.cntP*1000) : 0;
        const maxB = s.maxP * 1000;
        const avgC = s.cntN > 0 ? (s.sumN/s.cntN*1000) : 0;
        const minC = s.minN * 1000;
        const name = (regionNames[rid]||('r'+rid));
        const bSt = maxB > 30 ? 'WARN' : maxB > 15 ? 'OK' : 'GOOD';
        const cSt = minC < -30 ? 'WARN' : minC < -15 ? 'OK' : 'GOOD';
        console.log('    ' + name.padEnd(14) + 'bulge: avg=' + avgB.toFixed(1).padStart(6) + 'mm max=' +
          maxB.toFixed(1).padStart(6) + 'mm (v' + s.maxPVert + ') ' + bSt + '  cave: avg=' + avgC.toFixed(1).padStart(7) +
          'mm max=' + minC.toFixed(1).padStart(7) + 'mm (v' + s.minNVert + ') ' + cSt);
      }
      console.log('=== END SURFACE HEALTH ===');
    }

    meshGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
    if ((debugRegions || showStrain || showBoneError || showSurfaceHealth) && colors) meshGeo.attributes.color.needsUpdate = true;
    meshGeo.computeVertexNormals();
  }

  function updateCageMesh(){
    if(!cageMesh) return;
    const arr = cageMesh.geometry.attributes.position.array;
    for(let i=0;i<cageRest.length;i++){
      arr[i*3]   = cagePos[i*3];
      arr[i*3+1] = cagePos[i*3+1];
      arr[i*3+2] = cagePos[i*3+2];
    }
    cageMesh.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  }

  function applyCageSmoothing(){
    if(!ENABLE_CAGE_SMOOTHING || CAGE_SMOOTHING <= 0) return;
    if(!cageSmoothed || cageSmoothed.length !== cagePos.length){
      cageSmoothed = new Float32Array(cagePos.length);
      cageSmoothed.set(cagePos);
    }
    const s = clamp(CAGE_SMOOTHING, 0, 1);
    for(let i=0;i<cagePos.length;i++){
      cageSmoothed[i] = cageSmoothed[i] * (1 - s) + cagePos[i] * s;
      cagePos[i] = cageSmoothed[i];
    }
  }

  // ---------------- Foot plant correction ----------------
  function footLockCorrection(t){
    const plantStrength = +$('plant').value;
    if(plantStrength <= 0) { lockL=null; lockR=null; return; }

    const stanceThresh = +$('stanceKnee').value; // degrees
    const lKdeg = Math.abs(getRotationAtTime('l_knee', t, 'x_rot') * 180/Math.PI);
    const rKdeg = Math.abs(getRotationAtTime('r_knee', t, 'x_rot') * 180/Math.PI);

    const lStance = lKdeg < stanceThresh;
    const rStance = rKdeg < stanceThresh;

    const lFootC = regionCenter(R.l_foot);
    const rFootC = regionCenter(R.r_foot);

    if(lStance && !lockL) lockL = lFootC.slice();
    if(!lStance) lockL = null;

    if(rStance && !lockR) lockR = rFootC.slice();
    if(!rStance) lockR = null;

    if(lockL){
      const dx = (lockL[0]-lFootC[0]) * plantStrength;
      const dy = (lockL[1]-lFootC[1]) * plantStrength;
      const dz = (lockL[2]-lFootC[2]) * plantStrength;
      translate([].concat(R.l_thigh, R.l_shin, R.l_foot), dx,dy,dz);
    }
    if(lockR){
      const dx = (lockR[0]-rFootC[0]) * plantStrength;
      const dy = (lockR[1]-rFootC[1]) * plantStrength;
      const dz = (lockR[2]-rFootC[2]) * plantStrength;
      translate([].concat(R.r_thigh, R.r_shin, R.r_foot), dx,dy,dz);
    }
  }

  // ---------------- Foot plant correction (FBX position mode) ----------------
  // Detects planted feet via world-space ankle velocity from FBX joint data.
  // When a foot has near-zero velocity, locks it in place to prevent sliding.
  function footLockCorrectionFBX(t) {
    const plantStrength = +$('plant').value;
    if (plantStrength <= 0 || !jointPosData) { lockL = null; lockR = null; return; }
    if (!R.l_foot || !R.r_foot) return;

    const dur = jointPosData.duration;
    const dt = Math.min(0.02, dur * 0.01); // sample offset for velocity estimation

    // World-space ankle: root_position + hips-centered ankle
    const root0 = getRootPositionAtTime(t);
    const root1 = getRootPositionAtTime(t - dt);
    const lAnk0 = getJointPositionAtTime('l_ankle', t);
    const lAnk1 = getJointPositionAtTime('l_ankle', t - dt);
    const rAnk0 = getJointPositionAtTime('r_ankle', t);
    const rAnk1 = getJointPositionAtTime('r_ankle', t - dt);
    if (!root0 || !root1 || !lAnk0 || !lAnk1 || !rAnk0 || !rAnk1) return;

    // World-space velocities (per second)
    const lVx = ((root0[0]+lAnk0[0])-(root1[0]+lAnk1[0])) / dt;
    const lVy = ((root0[1]+lAnk0[1])-(root1[1]+lAnk1[1])) / dt;
    const lVz = ((root0[2]+lAnk0[2])-(root1[2]+lAnk1[2])) / dt;
    const rVx = ((root0[0]+rAnk0[0])-(root1[0]+rAnk1[0])) / dt;
    const rVy = ((root0[1]+rAnk0[1])-(root1[1]+rAnk1[1])) / dt;
    const rVz = ((root0[2]+rAnk0[2])-(root1[2]+rAnk1[2])) / dt;
    const lSpeed = Math.sqrt(lVx*lVx + lVy*lVy + lVz*lVz);
    const rSpeed = Math.sqrt(rVx*rVx + rVy*rVy + rVz*rVz);

    // Threshold: 5% of mesh height per second = practically stationary
    const velThresh = meshHeight * 0.05;
    const lStance = lSpeed < velThresh;
    const rStance = rSpeed < velThresh;

    const lFootC = regionCenter(R.l_foot);
    const rFootC = regionCenter(R.r_foot);

    if (lStance && !lockL) lockL = lFootC.slice();
    if (!lStance) lockL = null;
    if (rStance && !lockR) lockR = rFootC.slice();
    if (!rStance) lockR = null;

    if (lockL) {
      const dx = (lockL[0]-lFootC[0]) * plantStrength;
      const dy = (lockL[1]-lFootC[1]) * plantStrength;
      const dz = (lockL[2]-lFootC[2]) * plantStrength;
      translate([].concat(R.l_thigh, R.l_shin, R.l_foot), dx, dy, dz);
    }
    if (lockR) {
      const dx = (lockR[0]-rFootC[0]) * plantStrength;
      const dy = (lockR[1]-rFootC[1]) * plantStrength;
      const dz = (lockR[2]-rFootC[2]) * plantStrength;
      translate([].concat(R.r_thigh, R.r_shin, R.r_foot), dx, dy, dz);
    }
  }

  // ---------------- Cage driver: Mixamo -> cage ----------------
  function driveCageFromMixamo(t){
    // Spine direction (hoisted for head correction — set in position-mode body rotation)
    let restSpineDir = [0, 1, 0], curSpineDir = [0, 1, 0];
    // reset cage to rest
    for(let i=0;i<cageRest.length;i++){
      cagePos[i*3]=cageRest[i][0];
      cagePos[i*3+1]=cageRest[i][1];
      cagePos[i*3+2]=cageRest[i][2];
    }

    // All cage vertex indices for body-wide transforms
    const allIdx = [].concat(
      R.torso,
      R.pelvis || [],
      R.l_thigh, R.r_thigh,
      R.l_shin, R.r_shin,
      R.l_foot, R.r_foot,
      R.l_upperarm, R.r_upperarm,
      R.l_forearm, R.r_forearm,
      R.l_hand || [], R.r_hand || [],
      R.head
    );

    // Pelvis translation delta (stored for analytical pivot placement + bone comparison)
    pelvisDelta = [0, 0, 0];

    // ----- BODY motion (affects everything) -----
    if ($('enableBody').checked && P.pelvis) {
      if (jointPosData) {
        // Position mode: body ROTATIONS are already embedded in the hips-centered
        // joint positions (we only subtracted translation, not rotation).
        // Only apply pelvis TRANSLATION delta (bob/sway) from FBX root positions.
        const rootPos = getRootPositionAtTime(t);
        const restRoot = jointPosData.root_positions[0];
        if (rootPos && restRoot) {
          const hipBobScale = +$('hipBob').value;
          const hipSwayScale = +$('hipSway').value;
          // Scale by leg proportion ratio: if cage legs are shorter than FBX legs,
          // reduce pelvis bob/sway proportionally so the movement fits the cage's
          // actual leg reach and doesn't drive feet through the floor.
          const pr = fbxLegPropRatio;
          const dy = (rootPos[1] - restRoot[1]) * hipBobScale * pr;
          const dx = (rootPos[0] - restRoot[0]) * hipSwayScale * pr;
          const dz = (rootPos[2] - restRoot[2]) * hipSwayScale * pr;
          pelvisDelta = [dx, dy, dz];
          translate(allIdx, dx, dy, dz);
        }
      } else if (spineMotionData) {
        // Euler fallback: body motion from spine_keyframes.json
        const hipBobScale = +$('hipBob').value;
        const hipSwayScale = +$('hipSway').value;
        const forwardLean = +$('forwardLean').value * Math.PI/180;
        const spineTwistScale = +$('spineTwist').value;

        const rawBob = getSpineDataAtTime('hips', t, 'y_trans');
        const hipBob = (rawBob - 0.4) * hipBobScale;

        const hipSwayZ = getSpineDataAtTime('hips', t, 'z_rot') * Math.PI/180 * hipSwayScale;
        const hipTwistY = getSpineDataAtTime('hips', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180 * hipSwayScale;
        const spineTwist = getSpineDataAtTime('spine1', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180 * spineTwistScale;

        applyRotX(allIdx, P.pelvis, forwardLean);
        applyRotZ(allIdx, P.pelvis, hipSwayZ);
        applyRotY(allIdx, P.pelvis, hipTwistY);
        translate(allIdx, 0, hipBob, 0);

        // upper body counter twist (torso + head + arms)
        const upperIdx = [].concat(R.torso, R.head, R.l_upperarm, R.r_upperarm, R.l_forearm, R.r_forearm, R.l_hand || [], R.r_hand || []);
        const spineP = [P.pelvis[0], P.pelvis[1]+0.10, P.pelvis[2]];
        applyRotY(upperIdx, spineP, spineTwist);
      }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // POSITION-BASED DRIVING (when joint_positions.json is loaded)
    // Bypasses all Euler decomposition — uses hips-local joint positions
    // directly, with 2-segment IK for each limb.
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    if (jointPosData) {
      // Get hips-local positions for all joints at time t
      const jp = {};
      for (const jn of Object.keys(jointPosData.joints)) {
        jp[jn] = getJointPositionAtTime(jn, t);
      }

      // --- Body rotation from FBX spine direction ---
      // Compute rotation from rest spine direction to current spine direction.
      // Apply to ALL cage verts so torso Kabsch shares body rotation with limbs,
      // preventing transform discontinuity at body-limb boundaries.
      let bodyAxis = [0, 1, 0], bodyAngle = 0;
      const rpChest = jointPosData.rest_pose.chest;
      const rpPelvis = jointPosData.rest_pose.pelvis || [0, 0, 0];
      if (rpChest && jp.chest) {
        restSpineDir = vnorm(vsub(rpChest, rpPelvis));
        curSpineDir = vnorm(vsub(jp.chest, jp.pelvis || [0, 0, 0]));
        const bAxis = vcross(restSpineDir, curSpineDir);
        const bAxisLen = vlen(bAxis);
        const bDot = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(restSpineDir, curSpineDir)));
        bodyAngle = Math.acos(bDot);
        if (bAxisLen > 1e-6 && Math.abs(bodyAngle) > 1e-4) {
          bodyAxis = vnorm(bAxis);
          applyRotAxis(allIdx, P.pelvis, bodyAxis[0], bodyAxis[1], bodyAxis[2], bodyAngle);
        } else {
          bodyAngle = 0;
        }
      }

      // --- Spine twist from shoulder axis ---
      // After lean, extract the twist component: rotation around the spine axis
      // detected from the change in shoulder-to-shoulder direction.
      let twistAngle = 0;
      const rpLS_tw = jointPosData.rest_pose.l_shoulder;
      const rpRS_tw = jointPosData.rest_pose.r_shoulder;
      if (rpLS_tw && rpRS_tw && jp.l_shoulder && jp.r_shoulder) {
        const restShAxis = vnorm(vsub(rpRS_tw, rpLS_tw));
        const curShAxis = vnorm(vsub(jp.r_shoulder, jp.l_shoulder));
        // Lean-rotate the rest shoulder axis
        let leanedRestSh = restShAxis;
        if (Math.abs(bodyAngle) > 1e-4) {
          const r = rotateAroundAxis(restShAxis[0], restShAxis[1], restShAxis[2], 0, 0, 0,
            bodyAxis[0], bodyAxis[1], bodyAxis[2], bodyAngle);
          leanedRestSh = vnorm(r);
        }
        // Project both onto plane perpendicular to current spine direction
        const dotR = vdot(leanedRestSh, curSpineDir);
        const dotC = vdot(curShAxis, curSpineDir);
        let projR = vsub(leanedRestSh, vscale(curSpineDir, dotR));
        let projC = vsub(curShAxis, vscale(curSpineDir, dotC));
        if (vlen(projR) > 1e-6 && vlen(projC) > 1e-6) {
          projR = vnorm(projR);
          projC = vnorm(projC);
          const dotP = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(projR, projC)));
          twistAngle = Math.acos(dotP);
          const cross = vcross(projR, projC);
          if (vdot(cross, curSpineDir) < 0) twistAngle = -twistAngle;
          // Apply twist scale from UI slider
          const twistScale = +$('spineTwist').value;
          twistAngle *= twistScale;
        }
      }
      // Apply spine twist to upper body cage verts only (not legs)
      if (Math.abs(twistAngle) > 1e-4) {
        const upperIdx = [].concat(R.torso, R.head,
          R.l_upperarm, R.r_upperarm, R.l_forearm, R.r_forearm,
          R.l_hand || [], R.r_hand || []);
        applyRotAxis(upperIdx, P.pelvis, curSpineDir[0], curSpineDir[1], curSpineDir[2], twistAngle);
      }

      // --- Hip twist from hip-to-hip axis ---
      // Same approach as spine twist but from pelvis rotation detected via hip joint axis.
      // Applied to lower body only (thighs, shins, feet).
      let hipTwistAngle = 0;
      const rpLH_tw = jointPosData.rest_pose.l_hip;
      const rpRH_tw = jointPosData.rest_pose.r_hip;
      if (rpLH_tw && rpRH_tw && jp.l_hip && jp.r_hip) {
        const restHipAxis = vnorm(vsub(rpRH_tw, rpLH_tw));
        const curHipAxis = vnorm(vsub(jp.r_hip, jp.l_hip));
        let leanedRestHip = restHipAxis;
        if (Math.abs(bodyAngle) > 1e-4) {
          const r = rotateAroundAxis(restHipAxis[0], restHipAxis[1], restHipAxis[2], 0, 0, 0,
            bodyAxis[0], bodyAxis[1], bodyAxis[2], bodyAngle);
          leanedRestHip = vnorm(r);
        }
        const dotR = vdot(leanedRestHip, curSpineDir);
        const dotC = vdot(curHipAxis, curSpineDir);
        let projR = vsub(leanedRestHip, vscale(curSpineDir, dotR));
        let projC = vsub(curHipAxis, vscale(curSpineDir, dotC));
        if (vlen(projR) > 1e-6 && vlen(projC) > 1e-6) {
          projR = vnorm(projR);
          projC = vnorm(projC);
          const dotP = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(projR, projC)));
          hipTwistAngle = Math.acos(dotP);
          const cross = vcross(projR, projC);
          if (vdot(cross, curSpineDir) < 0) hipTwistAngle = -hipTwistAngle;
          const twistScale = +$('spineTwist').value;
          hipTwistAngle *= twistScale;
        }
      }
      // Apply hip twist to lower body cage verts (including pelvis)
      if (Math.abs(hipTwistAngle) > 1e-4) {
        const lowerIdx = [].concat(R.pelvis || [], R.l_thigh, R.r_thigh, R.l_shin, R.r_shin, R.l_foot, R.r_foot);
        applyRotAxis(lowerIdx, P.pelvis, curSpineDir[0], curSpineDir[1], curSpineDir[2], hipTwistAngle);
      }

      // Smooth hip-spine twist transition on lower torso cage verts.
      // Without this, there's a hard twist discontinuity between torso (spine twist
      // only) and legs (hip twist). For Capoeira with -0.15° spine vs -7° hip twist,
      // the 6.9° gap causes mesh tearing at the butt/crotch boundary.
      // Fix: blend the hip-spine difference into lower torso cage verts using a
      // height-based gradient, so torso Kabsch naturally captures some hip twist.
      const hipSpineDelta = hipTwistAngle - twistAngle;
      if (Math.abs(hipSpineDelta) > 1e-4 && R.torso) {
        const pelvisRestY = P.pelvis[1];
        const gradientH = 0.12; // blend over ~12cm above pelvis
        for (let i = 0; i < R.torso.length; i++) {
          const ci = R.torso[i];
          const restY = cageRest[ci][1];
          if (restY < pelvisRestY + gradientH) {
            const f = clamp(1 - (restY - pelvisRestY) / gradientH, 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            const partialTwist = hipSpineDelta * sf;
            if (Math.abs(partialTwist) > 1e-5) {
              const r = rotateAroundAxis(cagePos[ci*3], cagePos[ci*3+1], cagePos[ci*3+2],
                P.pelvis[0], P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2],
                curSpineDir[0], curSpineDir[1], curSpineDir[2], partialTwist);
              cagePos[ci*3] = r[0]; cagePos[ci*3+1] = r[1]; cagePos[ci*3+2] = r[2];
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Helper: rotate a direction vector by body rotation (lean + twist).
      // Uses spine twist for upper body (arms), hip twist for lower body (legs).
      function bodyRotDir(dir, isLeg) {
        let d = dir;
        if (Math.abs(bodyAngle) > 1e-4) {
          const r = rotateAroundAxis(d[0], d[1], d[2], 0, 0, 0,
            bodyAxis[0], bodyAxis[1], bodyAxis[2], bodyAngle);
          d = vnorm(r);
        }
        const tw = isLeg ? hipTwistAngle : twistAngle;
        if (Math.abs(tw) > 1e-4) {
          const r = rotateAroundAxis(d[0], d[1], d[2], 0, 0, 0,
            curSpineDir[0], curSpineDir[1], curSpineDir[2], tw);
          d = vnorm(r);
        }
        return d;
      }

      // One-shot diagnostic: log sample FBX positions at first frame
      if (!window._posDiagDone) {
        window._posDiagDone = true;
        console.log('=== POS-DRIVE DIAGNOSTIC at t=' + t.toFixed(3) + ' ===');
        console.log('Body rotation: angle=' + (bodyAngle * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(2) + '° axis=[' + bodyAxis.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') + ']');
        console.log('Spine twist: angle=' + (twistAngle * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(2) + '°');
        console.log('Hip twist: angle=' + (hipTwistAngle * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(2) + '°');
        console.log('FBX joint positions (hips-centered, X-mirrored):');
        for (const jn of ['pelvis','l_hip','l_knee','l_ankle','r_hip','r_knee','r_ankle','l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_wrist','r_shoulder','r_elbow','r_wrist','chest','neck','head']) {
          if (jp[jn]) console.log('  ' + jn + ': [' + jp[jn].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
        }
        console.log('Cage pivots (rest pose):');
        for (const pn of ['pelvis','l_hip','l_knee','l_ankle','r_hip','r_knee','r_ankle','l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_wrist','r_shoulder','r_elbow','r_wrist','neck','head']) {
          if (P[pn]) console.log('  P.' + pn + ': [' + P[pn].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
        }
        // Root position delta
        const rootPos = getRootPositionAtTime(t);
        const restRoot = jointPosData.root_positions[0];
        if (rootPos && restRoot) {
          console.log('Root pos at t: [' + rootPos.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
          console.log('Root pos rest: [' + restRoot.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
