    if ((allLeftArmCage.length || allRightArmCage.length) && R.torso && R.torso.length > 10) {
      const neckRY = P.neck ? P.neck[1] : P.pelvis[1] + 0.2;
      const midRY = (P.pelvis[1] + neckRY) / 2;
      // Compute deformed spine axis from torso cage vert centroids
      let bx = 0, by = 0, bz = 0, bn = 0; // lower torso centroid
      let ux = 0, uy = 0, uz = 0, un = 0; // upper torso centroid
      for (const ci of R.torso) {
        const ry = cageRest[ci][1];
        const px = cagePos[ci*3], py = cagePos[ci*3+1], pz = cagePos[ci*3+2];
        if (ry < midRY) { bx += px; by += py; bz += pz; bn++; }
        else { ux += px; uy += py; uz += pz; un++; }
      }
      if (bn > 0 && un > 0) {
        bx /= bn; by /= bn; bz /= bn;
        ux /= un; uy /= un; uz /= un;
        const capAx = ux - bx, capAy = uy - by, capAz = uz - bz;
        const capLen = Math.sqrt(capAx*capAx + capAy*capAy + capAz*capAz);
        if (capLen > 0.005) {
          const nax = capAx/capLen, nay = capAy/capLen, naz = capAz/capLen;
          // Capsule radius: compute from average torso vert distance to spine axis
          let radSum = 0, radN = 0;
          for (const ci of R.torso) {
            const px = cagePos[ci*3]-bx, py = cagePos[ci*3+1]-by, pz = cagePos[ci*3+2]-bz;
            const proj = px*nax + py*nay + pz*naz;
            const perpX = px - proj*nax, perpY = py - proj*nay, perpZ = pz - proj*naz;
            const perpD = Math.sqrt(perpX*perpX + perpY*perpY + perpZ*perpZ);
            radSum += perpD; radN++;
          }
          // Use 85% of average distance as collision radius + 5% margin beyond surface.
          // 60% was too conservative — arms passing through outer torso weren't detected.
          const avgR = (radN > 0) ? (radSum / radN) : 0.05;
          const capR = avgR * 0.85;
          const capRMargin = avgR * 0.90; // push verts slightly beyond surface to avoid clipping
          const capR2 = capRMargin * capRMargin;

          // Push arm verts outward if inside capsule
          const armIdx = [].concat(allLeftArmCage, allRightArmCage,
            R.l_hand || [], R.r_hand || []);
          let pushCount = 0;
          for (const ci of armIdx) {
            const px = cagePos[ci*3], py = cagePos[ci*3+1], pz = cagePos[ci*3+2];
            const dx = px - bx, dy = py - by, dz = pz - bz;
            let proj = dx*nax + dy*nay + dz*naz;
            proj = Math.max(0, Math.min(capLen, proj));
            const closestX = bx + nax*proj, closestY = by + nay*proj, closestZ = bz + naz*proj;
            const rx = px - closestX, ry2 = py - closestY, rz = pz - closestZ;
            const d2 = rx*rx + ry2*ry2 + rz*rz;
            if (d2 < capR2 && d2 > 1e-10) {
              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
              const push = capRMargin - d;
              const invD = 1 / d;
              cagePos[ci*3]   += rx * invD * push;
              cagePos[ci*3+1] += ry2 * invD * push;
              cagePos[ci*3+2] += rz * invD * push;
              pushCount++;
            }
          }
          // One-shot diagnostic (resets when FBX loaded via window._armCollisionDiag = false)
          if (!window._armCollisionDiag) {
            window._armCollisionDiag = true;
            console.log('=== ARM-TORSO COLLISION ===');
            console.log('  capsule: bottom=['+[bx,by,bz].map(v=>v.toFixed(4))+'] top=['+[ux,uy,uz].map(v=>v.toFixed(4))+'] len='+capLen.toFixed(4));
            console.log('  avg torso radius: '+avgR.toFixed(4)+' collision radius: '+capR.toFixed(4)+' margin: '+capRMargin.toFixed(4));
            console.log('  arm verts checked: '+armIdx.length+' pushed: '+pushCount);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // ----- HEAD/NECK motion -----
    // Position mode: handled analytically via headState (computed above).
    // No vert-level rotation needed — avoids pivot-mismatch at P.neck after body rotation.
    // Euler fallback: vert-level rotation from spine_keyframes.json.
    if (!jointPosData && spineMotionData && P.neck && P.head && R.head && R.head.length) {
      // Euler fallback: head from spine_keyframes.json
      const neckX = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'x_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const neckY = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const neckZ = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'z_rot') * Math.PI/180;

      applyRotX(R.head, P.neck, neckX);
      applyRotY(R.head, P.neck, neckY);
      applyRotZ(R.head, P.neck, neckZ);

      let headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        P.head[0], P.head[1], P.head[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 1,0,0, neckX);
      headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        headPivot[0], headPivot[1], headPivot[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 0,1,0, neckY);
      headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        headPivot[0], headPivot[1], headPivot[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 0,0,1, neckZ);

      const headX = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'x_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const headY = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const headZ = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'z_rot') * Math.PI/180;

      applyRotX(R.head, headPivot, headX);
      applyRotY(R.head, headPivot, headY);
      applyRotZ(R.head, headPivot, headZ);
    }

    // ----- Foot lock correction LAST (only with Euler data) -----
    // Foot lock: Euler mode uses knee angle detection, FBX mode uses ankle velocity
    if (!jointPosData) footLockCorrection(t);
    else footLockCorrectionFBX(t);

    // ----- Ground compensation (position mode) -----
    // When FBX skeleton has different proportions than the cage (e.g. longer legs),
    // the body lean + shorter cage legs = feet float above ground.
    // Fix: find lowest foot cage vert, compare to rest-pose lowest, and push down.
    if (jointPosData && R.l_foot && R.r_foot) {
      let minFootY = Infinity;
      const footIdx = [].concat(R.l_foot, R.r_foot);
      for (const ci of footIdx) {
        const y = cagePos[ci * 3 + 1];
        if (y < minFootY) minFootY = y;
      }
      // Rest-pose lowest foot vert
      let minFootRestY = Infinity;
      for (const ci of footIdx) {
        if (cageRest[ci][1] < minFootRestY) minFootRestY = cageRest[ci][1];
      }
      // Ground plane = rest-pose lowest foot vert Y.
      // groundCorrection < 0 → feet above ground (floating) → push down
      // groundCorrection > 0 → feet below ground (floor penetration) → push up
      // Both directions are corrected to keep feet at floor level.
      const groundCorrection = minFootRestY - minFootY;
      if (Math.abs(groundCorrection) > 0.001) {
        translate(allIdx, 0, groundCorrection, 0);
        lastGroundCorrection = groundCorrection;
      } else {
        lastGroundCorrection = 0;
      }
    } else {
      lastGroundCorrection = 0;
    }

    // One-shot: log ground comp + pivot alignment diagnostic
    if (!window._groundDiagDone && jointPosData) {
      window._groundDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== GROUND COMP + PIVOT DIAG ===');
      console.log('  lastGroundCorrection=' + lastGroundCorrection.toFixed(4));
      if (R.l_foot && R.r_foot) {
        const footIdx = [].concat(R.l_foot, R.r_foot);
        let minY = Infinity, minRestY = Infinity;
        for (const ci of footIdx) {
          if (cagePos[ci*3+1] < minY) minY = cagePos[ci*3+1];
          if (cageRest[ci][1] < minRestY) minRestY = cageRest[ci][1];
        }
        console.log('  foot cage minY=' + minY.toFixed(4) + ' rest minY=' + minRestY.toFixed(4));
      }
      const _mhL = legState && legState.movedHipL;
      const _mhR = legState && legState.movedHipR;
      if (_mhL) console.log('  movedHipL=[' + _mhL.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      if (_mhR) console.log('  movedHipR=[' + _mhR.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      console.log('  P.l_hip=[' + P.l_hip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      console.log('  P.r_hip=[' + P.r_hip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
    }

    applyCageSmoothing();
  }


function normalizeRegionId(regionIdInput, cageLen) {
  if (!Array.isArray(regionIdInput)) {
    console.warn("regions.regionId missing or invalid. Filling with zeros.");
    return new Array(cageLen).fill(0);
  }
  if (regionIdInput.length === cageLen) return regionIdInput.slice();
  if (regionIdInput.length > cageLen) {
    console.warn(`regions.regionId longer than cageRest (${regionIdInput.length} > ${cageLen}). Truncating.`);
    return regionIdInput.slice(0, cageLen);
  }
  console.warn(`regions.regionId shorter than cageRest (${regionIdInput.length} < ${cageLen}). Padding.`);
  const padded = regionIdInput.slice();
  const fillVal = regionIdInput.length ? regionIdInput[regionIdInput.length - 1] : 0;
  while (padded.length < cageLen) padded.push(fillVal);
  return padded;
}

const REGION_ADJ = {
  0: [0, 1, 2, 7, 8],   // torso can blend to upper legs/arms
  1: [1, 3, 0],         // l_thigh -> l_shin + torso
  2: [2, 4, 0],         // r_thigh -> r_shin + torso
  3: [3, 5, 1],         // l_shin -> l_foot + l_thigh
  4: [4, 6, 2],         // r_shin -> r_foot + r_thigh
  5: [5, 3],            // l_foot -> l_shin
  6: [6, 4],            // r_foot -> r_shin
  7: [7, 9, 0],         // l_upperarm -> l_forearm + torso
  8: [8, 10, 0],        // r_upperarm -> r_forearm + torso
  9: [9, 7, 12],        // l_forearm -> l_upperarm + l_hand
  10: [10, 8, 13],      // r_forearm -> r_upperarm + r_hand
  11: [11],             // head/neck (if present)
  12: [12, 9],          // l_hand -> l_forearm
  13: [13, 10]          // r_hand -> r_forearm
};

function classifyMeshRegion(meta, x, y, z){
  // Always return null to use nearest-cage-vert classification.
  // The cage regions are properly classified using joint positions (regenerate_cage.py).
  // Assigning each mesh vert the region of its nearest cage vert guarantees consistency.
  return null;
}

function buildBindKNNRegion(meshRestPos, cageRest, regionIdInput, regionMeta, K = 8) {
  const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
  const nCage = cageRest.length;
  const regionIdLocal = normalizeRegionId(regionIdInput, nCage);

  const regionToIndices = new Map();
  for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) {
    const r = regionIdLocal[ci];
    if (!regionToIndices.has(r)) regionToIndices.set(r, []);
    regionToIndices.get(r).push(ci);
  }

  const idx = new Int32Array(nMesh * K);
  const w   = new Float32Array(nMesh * K);
  let reclassCount = 0, clampCount = 0;
  let thighReclassCount = 0, thighClampCount = 0;

  for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
    const x = meshRestPos[vi*3], y = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], z = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];

    let meshRegion = classifyMeshRegion(regionMeta, x, y, z);
    if (meshRegion === null) {
      let nearestRegion = 0;
      let nearestD2 = 1e30;
      for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) {
        const cx = cageRest[ci][0], cy = cageRest[ci][1], cz = cageRest[ci][2];
        const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy, dz = z - cz;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;
        if (d2 < nearestD2) {
          nearestD2 = d2;
          nearestRegion = regionIdLocal[ci];
        }
      }
      meshRegion = nearestRegion;
    }

    let allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];

    // Geometry-based arm detection + torso clamp (Fix A + Fix B).
    // Catches bicep verts misclassified as torso by nearest-cage-vert.
    // Uses point-to-arm-axis distance + projection range to detect arm verts,
    // then clamps torso influence past a shoulder band.
    if (cageShoulderL && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirL && cageArmDirR) {
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const faLen = +$('forearmLen').value;
      const totalArmLen = uaLen + faLen;
      const ARM_RADIUS = 0.06;              // 6cm from axis = "on the arm"
      const SHOULDER_BAND = 0.15 * uaLen;   // torso blending within wider band for smoother shoulder
      const ELBOW_BAND = 0.12 * uaLen;      // cross-arm blending zone around elbow (smoothstep-weighted)

      // Check left arm
      const lsh = cageShoulderL, ld = cageArmDirL;
      const ltProj = (x-lsh[0])*ld[0] + (y-lsh[1])*ld[1] + (z-lsh[2])*ld[2];
      if (ltProj > 0 && ltProj < totalArmLen) {
        const lpx = lsh[0]+ld[0]*ltProj, lpy = lsh[1]+ld[1]*ltProj, lpz = lsh[2]+ld[2]*ltProj;
        const lAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-lpx)*(x-lpx) + (y-lpy)*(y-lpy) + (z-lpz)*(z-lpz));
        if (lAxDist < ARM_RADIUS) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = (ltProj <= uaLen) ? 7 : 9;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            reclassCount++;
          }
          // Shoulder clamp: no torso past shoulder band
          if (ltProj > SHOULDER_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: upper arm verts far from elbow -> no forearm
          if (meshRegion === 7 && ltProj < uaLen - ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 9);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: forearm verts far from elbow -> no upper arm
          if (meshRegion === 9 && ltProj > uaLen + ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 7);
            clampCount++;
          }
        }
      }

      // Check right arm
      const rsh = cageShoulderR, rd = cageArmDirR;
      const rtProj = (x-rsh[0])*rd[0] + (y-rsh[1])*rd[1] + (z-rsh[2])*rd[2];
      if (rtProj > 0 && rtProj < totalArmLen) {
        const rpx = rsh[0]+rd[0]*rtProj, rpy = rsh[1]+rd[1]*rtProj, rpz = rsh[2]+rd[2]*rtProj;
        const rAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-rpx)*(x-rpx) + (y-rpy)*(y-rpy) + (z-rpz)*(z-rpz));
        if (rAxDist < ARM_RADIUS) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = (rtProj <= uaLen) ? 8 : 10;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            reclassCount++;
          }
          if (rtProj > SHOULDER_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: upper arm far from elbow -> no forearm
          if (meshRegion === 8 && rtProj < uaLen - ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 10);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: forearm far from elbow -> no upper arm
          if (meshRegion === 10 && rtProj > uaLen + ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 8);
            clampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Geometry-based thigh detection + torso clamp (same pattern as arms).
    // Catches groin/upper-thigh verts misclassified as torso by nearest-cage-vert.
    // Without this, the cage's near-vertical right thigh (3° outward) means r_thigh
    // cage verts are so close to torso that KNN assigns zero thigh weight to groin mesh verts.
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_hip && P.r_knee) {
      // Left thigh
      const lh = P.l_hip, lk = P.l_knee;
      const lThLen = Math.sqrt((lk[0]-lh[0])**2 + (lk[1]-lh[1])**2 + (lk[2]-lh[2])**2);
      const lThD = [(lk[0]-lh[0])/lThLen, (lk[1]-lh[1])/lThLen, (lk[2]-lh[2])/lThLen];
      const THIGH_RADIUS = 0.25 * lThLen; // proportional to thigh length (~10cm)
      const THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_L = 0.55 * lThLen; // wider near hip for crotch/groin
      const lHipBand = 0.15 * lThLen;
      const HIP_EXTEND_L = 0.12 * lThLen; // detect slightly above hip for crotch
      const lThProj = (x-lh[0])*lThD[0] + (y-lh[1])*lThD[1] + (z-lh[2])*lThD[2];
      if (lThProj > -HIP_EXTEND_L && lThProj < lThLen) {
        const projCL = Math.max(0, lThProj);
        const lpx = lh[0]+lThD[0]*projCL, lpy = lh[1]+lThD[1]*projCL, lpz = lh[2]+lThD[2]*projCL;
        const lThAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-lpx)*(x-lpx) + (y-lpy)*(y-lpy) + (z-lpz)*(z-lpz));
        // Tapered radius: wide near hip (crotch), normal further down thigh
        const hipFracL = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 1 - lThProj / (0.3 * lThLen)));
        const effectiveRadiusL = THIGH_RADIUS + (THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_L - THIGH_RADIUS) * hipFracL;
        if (lThAxDist < effectiveRadiusL) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = 1;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            thighReclassCount++;
          }
          if (lThProj > lHipBand) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            thighClampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
      // Right thigh
      const rh = P.r_hip, rk = P.r_knee;
      const rThLen = Math.sqrt((rk[0]-rh[0])**2 + (rk[1]-rh[1])**2 + (rk[2]-rh[2])**2);
      const rThD = [(rk[0]-rh[0])/rThLen, (rk[1]-rh[1])/rThLen, (rk[2]-rh[2])/rThLen];
      const THIGH_RADIUS_R = 0.25 * rThLen;
      const THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_R = 0.55 * rThLen; // wider near hip for crotch/groin
      const rHipBand = 0.15 * rThLen;
      const HIP_EXTEND_R = 0.12 * rThLen; // detect slightly above hip for crotch
      const rThProj = (x-rh[0])*rThD[0] + (y-rh[1])*rThD[1] + (z-rh[2])*rThD[2];
      if (rThProj > -HIP_EXTEND_R && rThProj < rThLen) {
        const projCR = Math.max(0, rThProj);
        const rpx = rh[0]+rThD[0]*projCR, rpy = rh[1]+rThD[1]*projCR, rpz = rh[2]+rThD[2]*projCR;
        const rThAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-rpx)*(x-rpx) + (y-rpy)*(y-rpy) + (z-rpz)*(z-rpz));
        // Tapered radius: wide near hip (crotch), normal further down thigh
        const hipFracR = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 1 - rThProj / (0.3 * rThLen)));
        const effectiveRadiusR = THIGH_RADIUS_R + (THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_R - THIGH_RADIUS_R) * hipFracR;
        if (rThAxDist < effectiveRadiusR) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = 2;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            thighReclassCount++;
          }
          if (rThProj > rHipBand) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            thighClampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
      // Midline catch: inner-thigh verts between the two hip joints that fall
      // outside both cylindrical radii. These verts are close to the body midline
      // and neither thigh cylinder is wide enough to catch them. Without this,
      // torso cage verts remain in the KNN candidate pool → ~70% torso weight.
      // Handles BOTH cases:
      //   meshRegion === 0 (torso): reclassify to nearer thigh + clamp torso
      //   meshRegion === 1/2 (thigh but outside cylinder): just clamp torso
      {
        const lhx = lh[0], rhx = rh[0];
        const hipY = Math.max(lh[1], rh[1]);
        // Between the two hips in X and at/below hip level
        if (x > lhx && x < rhx && y < hipY + 0.02) {
          const midX = (lhx + rhx) / 2;
          const isL = x <= midX;
          const hip = isL ? lh : rh;
          const tLen = isL ? lThLen : rThLen;
          const td = isL ? lThD : rThD;
          const proj = ((x-hip[0])*td[0] + (y-hip[1])*td[1] + (z-hip[2])*td[2]);
          const hipBand = 0.15 * tLen;
          // Only past the hip band (not the crotch/groin area) and within thigh
          if (proj > hipBand && proj < tLen) {
            if (meshRegion === 0) {
              meshRegion = isL ? 1 : 2;
              allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
              thighReclassCount++;
            }
            // Clamp torso from allowed regions (catches both reclassified and
            // thigh-classified verts that the cylinder check missed)
            if (allowedRegions.includes(0)) {
              allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
              thighClampCount++;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const candidateIndices = [];
    for (const r of allowedRegions) {
      const list = regionToIndices.get(r);
      if (list) candidateIndices.push(...list);
    }

    if (!candidateIndices.length) {
      for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) candidateIndices.push(ci);
    }

    const bestD2 = new Float32Array(K);
    const bestI  = new Int32Array(K);
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) { bestD2[k] = 1e30; bestI[k] = candidateIndices[0] ?? 0; }

    for (const ci of candidateIndices) {
      const cx = cageRest[ci][0], cy = cageRest[ci][1], cz = cageRest[ci][2];
      const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy, dz = z - cz;
      const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;

      let worst = 0;
      for (let k = 1; k < K; k++) if (bestD2[k] > bestD2[worst]) worst = k;

      if (d2 < bestD2[worst]) {
        bestD2[worst] = d2;
        bestI[worst]  = ci;
      }
    }

    let sum = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const d = Math.sqrt(bestD2[k]) + 1e-6;
      const ww = 1.0 / d;
      w[vi*K + k] = ww;
      idx[vi*K + k] = bestI[k];
      sum += ww;
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) w[vi*K + k] /= sum;
  }

  if (reclassCount > 0 || clampCount > 0) {
    console.log('Arm geometry clamp: reclassified', reclassCount, 'torso→arm verts, clamped torso on', clampCount, 'arm verts');
  }
  if (thighReclassCount > 0 || thighClampCount > 0) {
    console.log('Thigh geometry clamp: reclassified', thighReclassCount, 'torso→thigh verts, clamped torso on', thighClampCount, 'thigh verts');
  }
  return { K, idx, w, regionId: regionIdLocal };
}

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // JOINT PLACEMENT SYSTEM — Mixamo-style drag from panel onto mesh
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  let placementMode = false;
  const placedMarkers = {};   // key → { el: HTMLElement, worldPos: [x,y,z], color: string }
  let draggedJoint = null;
  let dragSource = null;      // 'panel' or 'marker'
  let ghostEl = null;
  const jRaycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
  const jMouse = new THREE.Vector2();

  const JOINT_DEFS = {
    chin:     { mirror: null,      label: 'CHIN' },
    l_wrist:  { mirror: 'r_wrist', label: 'WRISTS' },
    l_elbow:  { mirror: 'r_elbow', label: 'ELBOWS' },
    l_knee:   { mirror: 'r_knee',  label: 'KNEES' },
    groin:    { mirror: null,      label: 'GROIN' },
    l_ankle:  { mirror: 'r_ankle', label: 'ANKLES' }
  };

  function mirrorX(pos) { return [-pos[0], pos[1], pos[2]]; }

  // Interior projection: find bone center from surface click
  function projectToInterior(hitPos, hitNormal) {
    if (!meshRestPos) return [hitPos.x, hitPos.y, hitPos.z];
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const sliceVal = hitPos.y;
    let bandVerts = [];
    for (const bw of [0.005, 0.015]) {
      bandVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        if (Math.abs(meshRestPos[i*3+1] - sliceVal) <= bw)
          bandVerts.push([meshRestPos[i*3], meshRestPos[i*3+1], meshRestPos[i*3+2]]);
      }
      if (bandVerts.length >= 10) break;
    }
    if (bandVerts.length < 3) {
      return [hitPos.x + hitNormal.x * -0.02, hitPos.y + hitNormal.y * -0.02, hitPos.z + hitNormal.z * -0.02];
    }
    const hp = [hitPos.x, hitPos.y, hitPos.z];
    let cx = 0, cy = 0, cz = 0;
    for (const v of bandVerts) { cx += v[0]; cy += v[1]; cz += v[2]; }
    cx /= bandVerts.length; cy /= bandVerts.length; cz /= bandVerts.length;
    let maxR = 0;
    for (const v of bandVerts) {
      const d = Math.sqrt((v[0]-cx)**2 + (v[1]-cy)**2 + (v[2]-cz)**2);
      if (d > maxR) maxR = d;
    }
    const nearVerts = bandVerts.filter(v => {
      const d = Math.sqrt((v[0]-hp[0])**2 + (v[1]-hp[1])**2 + (v[2]-hp[2])**2);
      return d < Math.max(maxR * 0.8, 0.03);
    });
    const useVerts = nearVerts.length >= 3 ? nearVerts : bandVerts;
    let fx = 0, fy = 0, fz = 0;
    for (const v of useVerts) { fx += v[0]; fy += v[1]; fz += v[2]; }
    return [fx / useVerts.length, fy / useVerts.length, fz / useVerts.length];
  }

  // Project interior pos outward to mesh surface
  function projectToSurface(interiorPos) {
    if (!meshObj) return interiorPos;
    const origin = new THREE.Vector3(interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1], interiorPos[2]);
    const dirs = [[0,0,1],[0,0,-1],[1,0,0],[-1,0,0],[0,1,0],[0,-1,0],
      [.707,0,.707],[-.707,0,.707],[.707,0,-.707],[-.707,0,-.707],[0,.5,.866],[0,-.5,.866]];
    const rc = new THREE.Raycaster();
    let bestDist = Infinity, bestPt = null;
    for (const d of dirs) {
      rc.set(origin, new THREE.Vector3(d[0],d[1],d[2]));
      const hits = rc.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0 && hits[0].distance < bestDist) { bestDist = hits[0].distance; bestPt = hits[0].point; }
    }
    return bestPt ? [bestPt.x, bestPt.y, bestPt.z] : [interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1], interiorPos[2] + 0.02];
  }

  // --- HTML overlay markers (always visible on top of mesh) ---
  function createPlacedMarkerEl(key, color) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'jp-placed';
    el.style.borderColor = color;
    el.style.background = color.replace(')', ',0.35)').replace('rgb', 'rgba');
    el.dataset.jointKey = key;
    const dot = document.createElement('div');
    dot.className = 'jp-dot';
    dot.style.background = color;
    el.appendChild(dot);
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    // Allow re-dragging placed markers
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
      if (!placementMode || !meshObj) return;
      draggedJoint = key;
      dragSource = 'marker'; // distinguish from panel drag
      ghostEl = null;        // no ghost needed, we move the marker directly
      controls.enabled = false;
      el.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    return el;
  }

  function placeMarkerHTML(key, worldPos, color) {
    if (placedMarkers[key]) {
      placedMarkers[key].worldPos = worldPos;
      updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
      return;
    }
    const el = createPlacedMarkerEl(key, color);
    placedMarkers[key] = { el, worldPos, color };
    updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
  }

  function updateMarkerScreenPos(key) {
    const m = placedMarkers[key];
    if (!m) return;
    const v = new THREE.Vector3(m.worldPos[0], m.worldPos[1], m.worldPos[2]);
    v.project(camera);
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.width + rect.left;
    const y = (-v.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.height + rect.top;
    m.el.style.left = x + 'px';
    m.el.style.top = y + 'px';
    m.el.style.display = (v.z > 1) ? 'none' : 'flex';  // hide if behind camera
  }

  function updateAllMarkerScreenPositions() {
    for (const key of Object.keys(placedMarkers)) updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
  }

  function removeMarkerHTML(key) {
    if (placedMarkers[key]) {
      if (placedMarkers[key].el.parentNode) placedMarkers[key].el.parentNode.removeChild(placedMarkers[key].el);
      delete placedMarkers[key];
    }
  }

  function clearAllMarkers() {
    for (const key of Object.keys(placedMarkers)) removeMarkerHTML(key);
  }

  function getRowColor(row) {
    const c = row.querySelector('.jp-circle');
    return c ? c.style.borderColor : '#fff';
  }

  // Apply joint placement to P
  function applyJointToP(key, interiorPos) {
    const useSymmetry = $('symmetryToggle').checked;
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
    if (key === 'chin') {
      P.neck = [interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1] - 0.01, interiorPos[2]];
    } else if (key === 'groin') {
      P.pelvis = interiorPos.slice();
    } else {
      P[key] = interiorPos.slice();
      if (def && def.mirror && useSymmetry) P[def.mirror] = mirrorX(interiorPos);
    }
  }

  // Build reverse lookup: mirror key → primary key (e.g., 'r_wrist' → 'l_wrist')
  const MIRROR_REVERSE = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(JOINT_DEFS)) {
    if (JOINT_DEFS[k].mirror) MIRROR_REVERSE[JOINT_DEFS[k].mirror] = k;
  }

  // Find the actual joint key when dragging a circle
  // Primary circles → left/center key; mirror circles → right key
  function getDragKey(row, circle) {
    const leftKey = row.dataset.joint;
    if (!circle.classList.contains('jp-mirror')) return leftKey;
    // Mirror circle → get the right-side key
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[leftKey];
    return def && def.mirror ? def.mirror : leftKey;
  }

  // Get the row for any joint key (left or right)
  function getRowForKey(key) {
    // Direct match
    let row = document.querySelector('.jp-row[data-joint="'+key+'"]');
    if (row) return row;
    // Reverse: key is a mirror target (e.g., 'r_wrist'), find the row for 'l_wrist'
    const primary = MIRROR_REVERSE[key];
    if (primary) return document.querySelector('.jp-row[data-joint="'+primary+'"]');
    return null;
  }

  // --- Drag from panel onto mesh ---
  // Get the mirror key for any joint key (works both directions)
  function getMirrorKey(key) {
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
    if (def && def.mirror) return def.mirror;       // left → right
    if (MIRROR_REVERSE[key]) return MIRROR_REVERSE[key]; // right → left
    return null;
  }

  // Place a joint on the mesh: set surface marker + update P + handle mirror
  function placeJointOnMesh(key, surfPt, hitNormal, color) {
    const interior = projectToInterior(
      new THREE.Vector3(surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]), hitNormal);

    // Place primary marker on surface
    placeMarkerHTML(key, surfPt, color);

    // Update P
    if (key === 'chin') {
      P.neck = [interior[0], interior[1] - 0.01, interior[2]];
    } else if (key === 'groin') {
      P.pelvis = interior.slice();
    } else {
      P[key] = interior.slice();
    }

    // Auto-mirror if symmetry is on
    if ($('symmetryToggle').checked) {
      const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(key);
      if (mirrorKey) {
        // Simple mirror: negate X of both surface point and interior
        const mirrorSurf = [-surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]];
        const mirrorInt = mirrorX(interior);
        placeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey, mirrorSurf, color);
        if (mirrorKey === 'chin' || mirrorKey === 'groin') {
          // these don't have a P key matching the mirror key
        } else {
          P[mirrorKey] = mirrorInt.slice();
        }
        // Mark mirror panel circle
        const row = getRowForKey(mirrorKey);
        if (row) {
          const mc = row.querySelector('.jp-mirror');
          const pc = row.querySelector('.jp-circle:not(.jp-mirror)');
          if (mc) mc.classList.add('placed');
          if (pc) pc.classList.add('placed');
        }
      }
    }

    // Mark panel circle as placed
    const row = getRowForKey(key);
    if (row) {
      const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
      if (def) {
        row.querySelector('.jp-circle:not(.jp-mirror)').classList.add('placed');
      } else if (MIRROR_REVERSE[key]) {
        const mc = row.querySelector('.jp-mirror');
        if (mc) mc.classList.add('placed');
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Drag from panel ---
  function onJointPointerDown(event) {
    if (!placementMode || !meshObj) return;
    const row = event.target.closest('.jp-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const circle = event.target.closest('.jp-circle');
    if (!circle) return;
    const key = getDragKey(row, circle);
    if (!key) return;

    draggedJoint = key;
    dragSource = 'panel';
    circle.classList.add('dragging');

    // Create ghost circle following cursor
    ghostEl = document.createElement('div');
    ghostEl.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;border:3px solid ' +
      circle.style.borderColor + ';background:' + circle.style.background +
      ';pointer-events:none;z-index:1000;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center';
    const dot = document.createElement('div');
    dot.style.cssText = 'width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:' + circle.style.borderColor;
    ghostEl.appendChild(dot);
    document.body.appendChild(ghostEl);
    ghostEl.style.left = event.clientX + 'px';
    ghostEl.style.top = event.clientY + 'px';

    controls.enabled = false;
    event.preventDefault();
  }

  function onDocPointerMove(event) {
    if (!draggedJoint) return;

    // Move ghost if panel drag
    if (ghostEl) {
      ghostEl.style.left = event.clientX + 'px';
      ghostEl.style.top = event.clientY + 'px';
    }

    // Raycast to mesh surface for preview
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right &&
        event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom) {
      jMouse.x =  ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      jMouse.y = -((event.clientY - rect.top)  / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      jRaycaster.setFromCamera(jMouse, camera);
      const hits = jRaycaster.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0) {
        const surfPt = [hits[0].point.x, hits[0].point.y, hits[0].point.z];
        const hitN = hits[0].face ? hits[0].face.normal.clone().transformDirection(meshObj.matrixWorld) : new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1);
        const row = getRowForKey(draggedJoint);
        const color = row ? getRowColor(row) : '#fff';

        // Preview: place marker at surface point
        placeMarkerHTML(draggedJoint, surfPt, color);

        // Preview mirror
        if ($('symmetryToggle').checked) {
          const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(draggedJoint);
          if (mirrorKey) {
            const mirrorSurf = [-surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]];
            placeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey, mirrorSurf, color);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function onDocPointerUp(event) {
    if (!draggedJoint) return;
    const key = draggedJoint;

    // Remove ghost
    if (ghostEl && ghostEl.parentNode) ghostEl.parentNode.removeChild(ghostEl);
    ghostEl = null;

    // Un-dim panel circles
    const row = getRowForKey(key);
    if (row) row.querySelectorAll('.jp-circle').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('dragging'));

    // Check if dropped on mesh
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right &&
        event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom) {
      jMouse.x =  ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      jMouse.y = -((event.clientY - rect.top)  / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      jRaycaster.setFromCamera(jMouse, camera);
      const hits = jRaycaster.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0) {
        const surfPt = [hits[0].point.x, hits[0].point.y, hits[0].point.z];
        const hitN = hits[0].face ? hits[0].face.normal.clone().transformDirection(meshObj.matrixWorld) : new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1);
        const color = row ? getRowColor(row) : '#fff';
        placeJointOnMesh(key, surfPt, hitN, color);
      }
    } else if (dragSource === 'panel') {
      // Dropped outside canvas from panel — cancel
      removeMarkerHTML(key);
      const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(key);
      if (mirrorKey) removeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey);
    }

    draggedJoint = null;
    dragSource = null;
    controls.enabled = true;
    // Reset cursor on placed markers
    document.querySelectorAll('.jp-placed').forEach(el => el.style.cursor = 'grab');
  }

  // Attach panel drag events
  document.getElementById('jointPanelLeft').addEventListener('pointerdown', onJointPointerDown);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove', onDocPointerMove);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup', onDocPointerUp);

  // Enter placement mode
  function startPlacement() {
    placementMode = true;
    draggedJoint = null;
    ghostEl = null;
    window._boneCompDiagDone = false;
    window._seamGapDiagDone = false;
    window._rShoulderDiag = false;
    $('play').checked = false;
    $('doneJoints').style.display = '';
    $('replaceJoints').style.display = 'none';
    $('jointPanel').style.display = '';
    document.querySelectorAll('.jp-circle').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('placed','dragging'));
    clearAllMarkers();
    $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Drag circles from the panel onto the mesh.';
  }

  // Exit placement mode, derive secondary joints, rebuild
  function finishPlacement() {
    placementMode = false;
    draggedJoint = null;
    $('doneJoints').style.display = 'none';
    $('replaceJoints').style.display = '';
    $('jointPanel').style.display = 'none';
    clearAllMarkers();

    // Derive shoulder from elbow + mesh geometry
    if (P.l_elbow) {
      let shoulderY = P.l_elbow[1];
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (meshRestPos[i*3] < -0.02 && meshRestPos[i*3+1] > maxY) maxY = meshRestPos[i*3+1];
        }
        shoulderY = maxY - 0.03;
      }
      const sX = P.l_elbow[0] * 0.35;
      P.l_shoulder = [sX, shoulderY, P.l_elbow[2]];
      P.r_shoulder = mirrorX(P.l_shoulder);
    }

    // Derive hips from groin
    if (P.pelvis) {
      let hipSpread = 0.08;
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (Math.abs(meshRestPos[i*3+1] - P.pelvis[1]) < 0.01) {
            minX = Math.min(minX, meshRestPos[i*3]); maxX = Math.max(maxX, meshRestPos[i*3]);
          }
        }
        if (maxX > minX) hipSpread = (maxX - minX) / 4;
      }
      P.l_hip = [P.pelvis[0] - hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
      P.r_hip = [P.pelvis[0] + hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
    }

    // Derive chest, head, toes, knuckles
    if (P.pelvis && P.neck) {
      P.chest = [P.pelvis[0], (P.pelvis[1] + P.neck[1]) * 0.5 + 0.03, P.pelvis[2]];
    }
    if (P.neck) {
      let headTop = P.neck[1] + 0.12;
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) { if (meshRestPos[i*3+1] > maxY) maxY = meshRestPos[i*3+1]; }
        headTop = maxY;
      }
      P.head = [P.neck[0], (P.neck[1] + headTop) * 0.5, P.neck[2]];
    }
    if (P.l_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.l_toe = [P.l_ankle[0], floorY, P.l_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }
    if (P.r_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.r_toe = [P.r_ankle[0], floorY, P.r_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }
    if (P.l_wrist && P.l_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      P.l_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.l_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }
    if (P.r_wrist && P.r_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.r_wrist, P.r_elbow));
      P.r_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.r_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }

    // Update sliders
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) {
      const tLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip));
      $('thighLen').value = tLen.toFixed(3); $('thighLenVal').textContent = tLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      const sLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
      $('shinLen').value = sLen.toFixed(3); $('shinLenVal').textContent = sLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) {
      const uLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder));
      $('upperArmLen').value = uLen.toFixed(3); $('upperArmLenVal').textContent = uLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
      const fLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      $('forearmLen').value = fLen.toFixed(3); $('forearmLenVal').textContent = fLen.toFixed(3);
    }

    // Update fbxLegPropRatio
    if (jointPosData && P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      const cageLeg = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
      const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose;
      if (rp.l_hip && rp.l_knee && rp.l_ankle) {
        const fbxLeg = vlen(vsub(rp.l_knee, rp.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_ankle, rp.l_knee));
        if (fbxLeg > 1e-6) fbxLegPropRatio = cageLeg / fbxLeg;
      }
    }

    // Validate bone lengths before saving
    const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
    const minBone = meshH * 0.07, maxBone = meshH * 0.50;
    const bonePairs = [
      ['l_hip','l_knee','l_thigh'], ['l_knee','l_ankle','l_shin'],
      ['l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_uarm'], ['l_elbow','l_wrist','l_farm']
    ];
    let badBones = [];
    for (const [a, b, name] of bonePairs) {
      if (P[a] && P[b]) {
        const len = vlen(vsub(P[b], P[a]));
        if (len < minBone || len > maxBone) badBones.push(name + '=' + len.toFixed(4));
      }
    }
    if (badBones.length > 0) {
      console.warn('WARNING: Bad bone lengths after placement: ' + badBones.join(', '));
      console.warn('Expected range: ' + minBone.toFixed(4) + ' - ' + maxBone.toFixed(4) + ' (meshH=' + meshH.toFixed(3) + ')');
      $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Warning: some bones look too short/long. Try re-placing.';
    }

    saveJointsToStorage();
    rebuildFromSkeleton();

    console.log('=== JOINT PLACEMENT COMPLETE ===');
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) console.log('  P.' + k + ' = [' + P[k].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
    }
    const lens = bonePairs.map(([a,b,n]) => P[a]&&P[b] ? n+'='+vlen(vsub(P[b],P[a])).toFixed(4) : '').filter(Boolean);
    console.log('  Bone lengths: ' + lens.join(', '));
    if (!badBones.length) $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Joints placed. Rebuild complete.';
  }

  // Save joint data to localStorage keyed by mesh identity
  function saveJointsToStorage() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    const data = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) data[k] = P[k].slice();
    }
    localStorage.setItem(meshKey, JSON.stringify({ P: data }));
    console.log('Saved joints to localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
  }

  // Load joint data from localStorage with sanity validation
  function loadJointsFromStorage() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return false;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(meshKey);
    if (!saved) return false;
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(saved);
      if (data.P) {
        // Sanity check: validate bone lengths relative to mesh height
        const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
        const minBone = meshH * 0.07;  // bone must be at least 7% of mesh height
        const maxBone = meshH * 0.50;  // bone must be less than 50% of mesh height
        const sp = data.P;
        const checks = [];
        if (sp.l_hip && sp.l_knee) checks.push({ name: 'l_thigh', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_knee, sp.l_hip)) });
        if (sp.l_knee && sp.l_ankle) checks.push({ name: 'l_shin', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_ankle, sp.l_knee)) });
        if (sp.l_shoulder && sp.l_elbow) checks.push({ name: 'l_uarm', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_elbow, sp.l_shoulder)) });
        if (sp.l_elbow && sp.l_wrist) checks.push({ name: 'l_farm', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_wrist, sp.l_elbow)) });
        let failed = [];
        for (const c of checks) {
          if (c.len < minBone || c.len > maxBone) failed.push(c.name + '=' + c.len.toFixed(4) + ' (range: ' + minBone.toFixed(4) + '-' + maxBone.toFixed(4) + ')');
        }
        if (failed.length > 0) {
          console.warn('Saved joints FAILED sanity check — discarding. Bad bones: ' + failed.join(', '));
          console.warn('Removing bad localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
          localStorage.removeItem(meshKey);
          return false;
        }
        for (const k of Object.keys(data.P)) {
          P[k] = data.P[k];
        }
        console.log('Restored joints from localStorage key: ' + meshKey + ' (all bone lengths OK)');
        return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Failed to parse saved joints:', e);
    }
    return false;
  }

  // Rebuild all region splits, bindings, and weights from current P
  function rebuildFromSkeleton() {
    if (!cageRest || !regionId || !meshRestPos) return;

    // Pelvis sub-region split
    const pelvisThreshY = P.pelvis[1] + 0.07;
    for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
      if (regionId[i] === 14) regionId[i] = 0;
    }
    let pelvisCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
      if (regionId[i] === 0 && cageRest[i][1] < pelvisThreshY) {
        regionId[i] = 14;
        pelvisCount++;
      }
    }

    R.torso = indicesByRegion(0);
    R.pelvis = indicesByRegion(14);
    R.l_thigh = indicesByRegion(1);
    R.r_thigh = indicesByRegion(2);
    R.l_shin  = indicesByRegion(3);
    R.r_shin  = indicesByRegion(4);
    R.l_foot  = indicesByRegion(5);
    R.r_foot  = indicesByRegion(6);
    R.l_upperarm = indicesByRegion(7);
    R.r_upperarm = indicesByRegion(8);
    R.l_forearm  = indicesByRegion(9);
    R.r_forearm  = indicesByRegion(10);
    R.head       = indicesByRegion(11);

    allLeftArmCage  = [].concat(R.l_upperarm, R.l_forearm);
    allRightArmCage = [].concat(R.r_upperarm, R.r_forearm);
    allLeftLegCage  = [].concat(R.l_thigh, R.l_shin, R.l_foot);
    allRightLegCage = [].concat(R.r_thigh, R.r_shin, R.r_foot);
    allPelvisCage = R.pelvis.slice();
    if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip) {
      const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
      for (const ci of R.torso) {
        if (cageRest[ci][1] <= hipY + 0.12) allPelvisCage.push(ci);
      }
    }

    regionIndices[0]  = R.torso;
    if (R.pelvis.length >= 4) regionIndices[14] = R.pelvis;
    regionIndices[1]  = R.l_thigh;
    regionIndices[2]  = R.r_thigh;
    regionIndices[3]  = R.l_shin;
    regionIndices[4]  = R.r_shin;
    regionIndices[5]  = R.l_foot;
    regionIndices[6]  = R.r_foot;
    if (R.head.length) regionIndices[11] = R.head;

    resplitArmRegions();
    resplitLegRegions();
    buildStretchConstraints();
    buildCrossBoundaryEdges();
    buildMeshAdjacency();
    computeRestVertexNormals();

    console.log('[REBUILD] Pelvis: ' + pelvisCount + ' verts, regions re-split, bindings rebuilt');
  }

  // Button handlers
  $('replaceJoints').addEventListener('click', startPlacement);
  $('doneJoints').addEventListener('click', finishPlacement);
  $('clearJoints').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    localStorage.removeItem(meshKey);
    $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Saved joints cleared. Reload page to use defaults.';
    console.log('Cleared saved joints from localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
  });

  // ---------------- Init ----------------
  (async function init(){
    statusEl.textContent = "Loading mesh.glb…";
    const gltf = await new Promise((res,rej)=> new THREE.GLTFLoader().load("mesh.glb", res, undefined, rej));
    gltf.scene.traverse(o=>{
      if(o.isMesh && !meshObj){
        meshObj=o;
        meshGeo=o.geometry;
        meshRestPos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array.slice();

        // Add vertex colors for debug region visualization
        const numVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        const colors = new Float32Array(numVerts * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < numVerts * 3; i++) colors[i] = 1.0;
        meshGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));

        if(o.material){ o.material.metalness=0; o.material.roughness=0.85; o.material.side=THREE.DoubleSide; o.material.vertexColors=false; o.material.needsUpdate=true; }
      }
    });
    scene.add(gltf.scene);

    const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(gltf.scene);
    const size = box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
    meshHeight = Math.max(1e-6, size.y);
    const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
    controls.target.copy(center);
    camera.position.set(center.x, center.y + 0.35*meshHeight, center.z + 1.6*meshHeight);
    controls.update();

    // Floor grid for visual reference
    const floorY = box.min.y;
    const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(2, 20, 0x888888, 0x444444);
    gridHelper.position.y = floorY;
    scene.add(gridHelper);
statusEl.textContent = "Loading cage/bind/regions/motion…";

let cage, bind, regions, anchorData, legMotion, armMotion, spineMotion;

try {
  [
    cage,
    bind,
    regions,
    anchorData,
    legMotion,
    armMotion,
    spineMotion
  ] = await Promise.all([
    loadJSON("cage.json"),
    loadJSON("bind.json"),
    loadJSON("regions.json"),
    loadJSON("discovered_anchors.json"),
    loadJSON("leg_keyframes.json"),
    loadJSON("arm_keyframes.json"),
    loadJSON("spine_keyframes.json")
  ]);
} catch (err) {
  console.error(err);
  statusEl.textContent = `Load failed: ${err.message}`;
  throw err; // stop init
}

// Try to load joint_positions.json (optional — enables position-based driving)
try {
  jointPosData = await loadJSON("joint_positions.json");
  console.log("Loaded joint_positions.json:", jointPosData.frame_count, "frames,",
    Object.keys(jointPosData.joints).length, "joints,",
    jointPosData.duration.toFixed(3) + "s @" + jointPosData.fps + "fps");
  // Use position data's duration as cycle duration
  cycleDuration = jointPosData.duration;
} catch (e) {
  console.log("No joint_positions.json found — using Euler-based driving");
  jointPosData = null;
}

// Debug
console.log("regionId length =", regions.regionId?.length);
console.log("region meta =", regions.meta);

    // cage
    cageRest = cage.verts;
    cageFaces = cage.faces;
    cagePos = new Float32Array(cageRest.length*3);
    for(let i=0;i<cageRest.length;i++){
      cagePos[i*3]=cageRest[i][0];
      cagePos[i*3+1]=cageRest[i][1];
      cagePos[i*3+2]=cageRest[i][2];
    }

    // regions (validate length to match cage vertices)
    regionId = normalizeRegionId(regions.regionId, cageRest.length);

    console.log("cageRest length =", cageRest.length);
    console.log("regions.regionId length =", regions.regionId?.length);
    console.log("mesh vert count =", meshRestPos.length / 3);

    // Cage symmetry diagnostic + auto-fix: detect L/R cage vertex count asymmetry
    // and mirror cage verts from the larger side to replace the deficient side.
    // This ensures symmetric deformation for all paired body parts.
    {
      const REGION_PAIRS = [
        [1, 2, 'thigh'], [3, 4, 'shin'], [5, 6, 'foot'],
        [7, 8, 'upperarm'], [9, 10, 'forearm'], [12, 13, 'hand']
      ];
      let totalFixed = 0;
      console.log('=== CAGE SYMMETRY DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      for (const [lRid, rRid, name] of REGION_PAIRS) {
        const lIdx = [], rIdx = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
          if (regionId[i] === lRid) lIdx.push(i);
          else if (regionId[i] === rRid) rIdx.push(i);
        }
        const diff = Math.abs(lIdx.length - rIdx.length);
        const status = diff === 0 ? 'SYMMETRIC' : diff <= 2 ? 'MINOR' : 'ASYMMETRIC';
        console.log('  ' + name + ': L=' + lIdx.length + ' R=' + rIdx.length +
          (diff > 0 ? ' (diff=' + diff + ') ' + status : ' ' + status));

        // Auto-fix: mirror from larger side to smaller side
        if (diff > 0) {
          const srcIdx = lIdx.length >= rIdx.length ? lIdx : rIdx;
          const dstIdx = lIdx.length >= rIdx.length ? rIdx : rIdx.length >= lIdx.length ? lIdx : rIdx;
          const srcRid = lIdx.length >= rIdx.length ? lRid : rRid;
          const dstRid = lIdx.length >= rIdx.length ? rRid : lRid;
          const isLtoR = lIdx.length > rIdx.length; // mirroring L→R

          // Sort source verts by projection along arm/leg axis for consistent pairing
          const srcSorted = srcIdx.slice().sort((a, b) => {
            const ay = cageRest[a][1], by = cageRest[b][1];
            return ay - by; // sort by Y (height)
          });

          if (srcSorted.length > dstIdx.length) {
            // Add mirrored cage verts for the extras
            const existing = dstIdx.length;
            for (let i = existing; i < srcSorted.length; i++) {
              const si = srcSorted[i];
              const sv = cageRest[si];
              // Mirror across X=0
              const mirrored = [-sv[0], sv[1], sv[2]];
              const newIdx = cageRest.length;
              cageRest.push(mirrored);
              regionId.push(dstRid);
              totalFixed++;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (totalFixed > 0) {
        console.log('  Fixed: added ' + totalFixed + ' mirrored cage verts for symmetry');
        // Rebuild cagePos flat array with new verts
        cagePos = new Float32Array(cageRest.length * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < cageRest.length; i++) {
          cagePos[i*3]   = cageRest[i][0];
          cagePos[i*3+1] = cageRest[i][1];
          cagePos[i*3+2] = cageRest[i][2];
        }
      }
      console.log('=== END CAGE SYMMETRY ===');
    }

    // TEMP TEST: ignore bind.json and build a clean bind (region constrained)
    const built = buildBindKNNRegion(meshRestPos, cageRest, regionId, regions.meta, 8);
    K = built.K;
    bindIdx = built.idx;
    bindW = built.w;
    regionId = built.regionId;

    // SAFE max calc (no spread)
    let maxBindIdx = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < bindIdx.length; i++) {
      if (bindIdx[i] > maxBindIdx) maxBindIdx = bindIdx[i];
    }
    if (maxBindIdx >= cageRest.length) {
      console.warn("bindIdx range exceeds cageRest length:", maxBindIdx, cageRest.length);
    }

    // Count mesh verts per region (from nearest-cage-vert classification)
    const meshRegionCounts = {};
    const nMeshVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    for (let vi = 0; vi < nMeshVerts; vi++) {
      // Find the dominant region for this mesh vert from its bindings
      const rw = new Map();
      for (let j = 0; j < K; j++) {
        const ci = bindIdx[vi*K+j];
        const w = bindW[vi*K+j];
        const rid = regionId[ci];
        rw.set(rid, (rw.get(rid)||0) + w);
      }
      let bestR = 0, bestW = -1;
      for (const [rid, w] of rw) { if (w > bestW) { bestR = rid; bestW = w; } }
      meshRegionCounts[bestR] = (meshRegionCounts[bestR]||0) + 1;
    }
    const names = {0:'torso',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
                   5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',11:'head'};
    console.log("Mesh vert region counts (dominant):");
    for (const rid of Object.keys(meshRegionCounts).sort((a,b)=>a-b)) {
      console.log(`  ${names[rid]||rid}: ${meshRegionCounts[rid]}`);
    }

    console.log("Built bind:", {
      K,
      cageVerts: cageRest.length,
      meshVerts: meshRestPos.length / 3,
      maxBindIdx
    });


    // anchors + bounds — MUST be set before resplitArmRegions() so that P is
    // available for thigh geometry detection and hip smoothstep in buildBindKNNRegion
    // and precomputeMeshRegionWeights. Previously these were set AFTER resplitArmRegions,
    // meaning all thigh detection and hip weight blending was silently skipped on load.
    anchors = anchorData.anchors;
    bounds = anchorData.mesh_bounds;

    // Pivots from anchors (CRITICAL — must be before mesh weight computation)
    P.l_hip   = (anchors.l_hip && anchors.l_hip.pos) ? anchors.l_hip.pos : [-0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.38, 0];
    P.r_hip   = (anchors.r_hip && anchors.r_hip.pos) ? anchors.r_hip.pos : [ 0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.38, 0];
    P.l_knee  = (anchors.l_knee && anchors.l_knee.pos) ? anchors.l_knee.pos : [-0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.22, 0.02];
    P.r_knee  = (anchors.r_knee && anchors.r_knee.pos) ? anchors.r_knee.pos : [ 0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.22, 0.02];
    P.l_ankle = (anchors.l_ankle && anchors.l_ankle.pos) ? anchors.l_ankle.pos : [-0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.06, 0.02];
    P.r_ankle = (anchors.r_ankle && anchors.r_ankle.pos) ? anchors.r_ankle.pos : [ 0.08, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.06, 0.02];
    P.pelvis  = (anchors.pelvis && anchors.pelvis.pos) ? anchors.pelvis.pos : [bounds.center_x, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.45, 0];
    // Derive shoulder/elbow/wrist proportional to mesh size
    {
      const meshH = bounds.height;
      const shoulderY = bounds.min[1] + meshH * 0.72;
      const shoulderX = meshH * 0.15;
      P.l_shoulder = [-shoulderX, shoulderY, -0.03];
      P.r_shoulder = [ shoulderX, shoulderY, -0.03];
      // Scale arm lengths to mesh height (defaults assume ~1.0 unit mesh)
      const ua = meshH * 0.19;
      const fa = meshH * 0.12;
      $('upperArmLen').value = ua.toFixed(2);
      $('upperArmLenVal').textContent = ua.toFixed(2);
      $('forearmLen').value = fa.toFixed(2);
      $('forearmLenVal').textContent = fa.toFixed(2);
      P.l_elbow = [P.l_shoulder[0] - ua, P.l_shoulder[1], P.l_shoulder[2]];
      P.r_elbow = [P.r_shoulder[0] + ua, P.r_shoulder[1], P.r_shoulder[2]];
      P.l_wrist = [P.l_elbow[0] - fa, P.l_elbow[1], P.l_elbow[2]];
      P.r_wrist = [P.r_elbow[0] + fa, P.r_elbow[1], P.r_elbow[2]];
      P.l_mid_knuckle = [P.l_wrist[0] - 0.04, P.l_wrist[1], P.l_wrist[2]];
      P.r_mid_knuckle = [P.r_wrist[0] + 0.04, P.r_wrist[1], P.r_wrist[2]];
      console.log('[ARM DEFAULTS] meshH=' + meshH.toFixed(3) + ' shoulderX=' + shoulderX.toFixed(3) + ' shoulderY=' + shoulderY.toFixed(3) + ' ua=' + ua.toFixed(3) + ' fa=' + fa.toFixed(3));
    }
    P.neck = (anchors.neck && anchors.neck.pos) ? anchors.neck.pos : [0, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.78, 0];
    P.head = (anchors.head && anchors.head.pos) ? anchors.head.pos : [0, bounds.min[1] + bounds.height*0.90, 0];
    // Derive chest position (midpoint between pelvis and neck, slightly above)
    P.chest = (anchors.chest && anchors.chest.pos) ? anchors.chest.pos
      : [P.pelvis[0], (P.pelvis[1] + P.neck[1]) * 0.5 + 0.03, P.pelvis[2]];
    // Derive toe positions (at floor level, slightly forward of ankle)
    {
      const floorY = bounds.min[1];
      P.l_toe = [P.l_ankle[0], floorY, P.l_ankle[2] + 0.04];
      P.r_toe = [P.r_ankle[0], floorY, P.r_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }

    // Override P from localStorage if user previously placed joints for this mesh
    if (loadJointsFromStorage()) {
      console.log('Using saved joint positions from localStorage');
      // Update sliders from restored joints
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) {
        const tLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip));
        $('thighLen').value = tLen.toFixed(3);
        $('thighLenVal').textContent = tLen.toFixed(3);
      }
      if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
        const sLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
        $('shinLen').value = sLen.toFixed(3);
        $('shinLenVal').textContent = sLen.toFixed(3);
      }
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) {
        const uLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder));
        $('upperArmLen').value = uLen.toFixed(3);
        $('upperArmLenVal').textContent = uLen.toFixed(3);
      }
      if (P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
        const fLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
        $('forearmLen').value = fLen.toFixed(3);
        $('forearmLenVal').textContent = fLen.toFixed(3);
      }
    }

    // Diagnostic: log all P joints and bone lengths
    {
      console.log('=== JOINT POSITIONS (P) ===');
      for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
        if (P[k]) console.log('  P.' + k + ' = [' + P[k].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
      }
      const boneLens = [];
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) boneLens.push('l_thigh=' + vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip)).toFixed(4));
      if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) boneLens.push('l_shin=' + vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee)).toFixed(4));
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) boneLens.push('l_uarm=' + vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder)).toFixed(4));
      if (P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) boneLens.push('l_farm=' + vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow)).toFixed(4));
      console.log('  Bone lengths: ' + boneLens.join(', '));
    }
    // Initialize retargetedJoints from rest-pose P (so skeleton overlay works before animation)
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) retargetedJoints[k] = P[k].slice();
    }

    // ── PELVIS SUB-REGION SPLIT ──
    // Reassign lower torso cage verts (near hips) to region 14 (pelvis).
    // This gives crotch mesh verts a stable local transform to blend with,
    // instead of the full-body torso transform that includes shoulder/chest motion.
    const pelvisThreshY = P.pelvis[1] + 0.07; // ~7cm above pelvis center
    let pelvisCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
      if (regionId[i] === 0 && cageRest[i][1] < pelvisThreshY) {
        regionId[i] = 14;
        pelvisCount++;
      }
    }
    console.log('[PELVIS SPLIT] Reassigned ' + pelvisCount + ' torso cage verts to pelvis region 14 (Y < ' + pelvisThreshY.toFixed(4) + ')');

    R.torso = indicesByRegion(0);
    R.pelvis = indicesByRegion(14);
    R.l_thigh = indicesByRegion(1);
    R.r_thigh = indicesByRegion(2);
    R.l_shin  = indicesByRegion(3);
    R.r_shin  = indicesByRegion(4);
    R.l_foot  = indicesByRegion(5);
    R.r_foot  = indicesByRegion(6);
    R.l_upperarm = indicesByRegion(7);
    R.r_upperarm = indicesByRegion(8);
    R.l_forearm  = indicesByRegion(9);
    R.r_forearm  = indicesByRegion(10);
    R.head       = indicesByRegion(11);

    // Save combined arm cage vert pools (before re-split)
    allLeftArmCage  = [].concat(R.l_upperarm, R.l_forearm);
    allRightArmCage = [].concat(R.r_upperarm, R.r_forearm);

    // Save combined leg cage vert pools (before re-split)
    allLeftLegCage  = [].concat(R.l_thigh, R.l_shin, R.l_foot);
    allRightLegCage = [].concat(R.r_thigh, R.r_shin, R.r_foot);
    // Pelvis pool: original pelvis verts + low torso verts near hip area
    // (so the pelvis slider can grow pelvis by pulling from torso)
    allPelvisCage = R.pelvis.slice();
    const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
    let lowTorsoCount = 0;
    for (const ci of R.torso) {
      if (cageRest[ci][1] <= hipY + 0.12) { // within 12cm above hip
        allPelvisCage.push(ci);
        lowTorsoCount++;
      }
    }
    console.log('Leg pools: L_leg=' + allLeftLegCage.length + ' R_leg=' + allRightLegCage.length +
      ' pelvis+lowTorso=' + allPelvisCage.length + ' (lowTorso=' + lowTorsoCount + ')');

    // Auto-compute leg slider defaults from cage geometry
    {
      const tLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip));
      const sLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
      $('thighLen').value = tLen.toFixed(3);
      $('thighLenVal').textContent = tLen.toFixed(3);
      $('shinLen').value = sLen.toFixed(3);
      $('shinLenVal').textContent = sLen.toFixed(3);
      console.log('Leg slider defaults: thigh=' + tLen.toFixed(4) + ' shin=' + sLen.toFixed(4));
    }

    console.log("Baked cage arm sizes: l_uarm=" + R.l_upperarm.length +
      " l_farm=" + R.l_forearm.length + " r_uarm=" + R.r_upperarm.length +
      " r_farm=" + R.r_forearm.length);
    console.log("Pelvis region: " + R.pelvis.length + " verts, torso remaining: " + R.torso.length);

    // Build regionIndices lookup for Kabsch transforms (non-arm regions)
    regionIndices[0]  = R.torso;
    if (R.pelvis.length >= 4) regionIndices[14] = R.pelvis; // need ≥4 for stable Kabsch
    regionIndices[1]  = R.l_thigh;
    regionIndices[2]  = R.r_thigh;
    regionIndices[3]  = R.l_shin;
    regionIndices[4]  = R.r_shin;
    regionIndices[5]  = R.l_foot;
    regionIndices[6]  = R.r_foot;
    if (R.head.length) regionIndices[11] = R.head;

    // Re-split arm cage verts by distance from shoulder (matching reference)
    // This also rebuilds mesh bindings and region weights
    resplitArmRegions();

    // Re-split leg cage verts using skeleton joint positions.
    // CRITICAL: must be called here on initial load — not just on slider input.
    // Without this, leg regions use the original cage file assignments instead of
    // skeleton-aware projection-based splits, AND the thigh geometry clamp in
    // buildBindKNNRegion gets correct leg region distribution.
    resplitLegRegions();

    // Build PBD stretch constraints for crotch edges (after weights + pivots ready)
    buildStretchConstraints();
    buildCrossBoundaryEdges();

    // Build mesh adjacency (edges + neighbor lists) for strain diagnostic + Delta Mush
    buildMeshAdjacency();
    computeRestVertexNormals();

    // Hook up slider to re-split when adjusted
    $('upperArmLen').addEventListener('input', resplitArmRegions);
    $('forearmLen').addEventListener('input', resplitArmRegions);
    $('shoulderX').addEventListener('input', resplitArmRegions);
    $('pelvisHeight').addEventListener('input', resplitLegRegions);
    $('thighLen').addEventListener('input', resplitLegRegions);
    $('shinLen').addEventListener('input', resplitLegRegions);

    // motion JSON
    motionData = legMotion.keyframes;
    // Only use leg duration if joint_positions.json didn't already set it
    if (!jointPosData) cycleDuration = legMotion.duration || 1.0;
    armMotionData = armMotion.keyframes || null;
    spineMotionData = spineMotion.keyframes || null;

    // Smooth spine and leg Euler rotation data (same as arm smoothing below).
    // These drive body lean, hip twist, and leg angles — noise here propagates everywhere.
    if (spineMotionData) {
      for (const boneName of Object.keys(spineMotionData)) {
        const bone = spineMotionData[boneName];
        if (!bone || typeof bone !== 'object') continue;
        for (const axis of ['x_rot', 'y_rot', 'z_rot']) {
          if (bone[axis] && Array.isArray(bone[axis]) && bone[axis].length > 3) {
            bone[axis] = smoothArray(bone[axis], 8);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (motionData) {
      for (const boneName of Object.keys(motionData)) {
        const bone = motionData[boneName];
        if (!bone || typeof bone !== 'object') continue;
        for (const axis of ['x_rot', 'y_rot', 'z_rot']) {
          if (bone[axis] && Array.isArray(bone[axis]) && bone[axis].length > 3) {
            bone[axis] = smoothArray(bone[axis], 8);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Post-process arm keyframe data for smooth animation.
    if (armMotionData) {
      // Smooth elbow/wrist data (8 passes removes micro-oscillations while
      // preserving the bend shape; elbows have more complex motion than shoulders).
      for (const boneName of Object.keys(armMotionData)) {
        if (boneName.includes('shoulder')) continue; // shoulders handled below
        const bone = armMotionData[boneName];
        for (const axis of ['x_rot', 'y_rot', 'z_rot']) {
          if (bone[axis] && bone[axis].length > 3) {
            bone[axis] = smoothArray(bone[axis], 8);
          }
        }
      }

      // Replace shoulder x_rot with sinusoidal fit for perfectly smooth pendulum.
      // Walking arm swing IS fundamentally a pendulum — the Mixamo data has a
      // 10-frame plateau/wobble region from Euler decomposition artifacts that
      // causes visible jerkiness. Fitting a cosine preserves timing and amplitude
      // while eliminating all micro-reversals.
      for (const boneName of ['l_shoulder', 'r_shoulder']) {
        const bone = armMotionData[boneName];
        if (!bone || !bone.x_rot || bone.x_rot.length < 4) continue;
        const arr = bone.x_rot;
        const n = arr.length;
        const meanVal = arr.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0) / n;
        const maxV = Math.max(...arr);
        const minV = Math.min(...arr);
        const amplitude = (maxV - minV) / 2;
        const peakIdx = arr.indexOf(maxV);
        // Replace with cosine: peaks at peakIdx, troughs half a cycle away
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          arr[i] = meanVal + amplitude * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * (i - peakIdx) / n);
        }
      }
    }

    // Compute arm baselines for delta computation.
    // Shoulder: MEAN-based baseline → symmetric forward/backward swing.
    // Elbow/Wrist: MIN-bend baseline → deltas are always >= 0, no hyperextension.
    //   Left z_rot is positive (more = more bend), so baseline = min(z_rot).
    //   Right z_rot is negative (more negative = more bend), so baseline = max(z_rot).
    if (armMotionData) {
      const deg = Math.PI / 180;
      const mean = (bone, axis) => {
        const d = armMotionData[bone];
        if (!d || !d[axis] || !d[axis].length) return 0;
        const arr = d[axis];
        return (arr.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0) / arr.length) * deg;
      };
      const first = (bone, axis) => {
        const d = armMotionData[bone];
        return d && d[axis] && d[axis].length ? d[axis][0] * deg : 0;
      };
      const minVal = (bone, axis) => {
        const d = armMotionData[bone];
        return d && d[axis] && d[axis].length ? Math.min(...d[axis]) * deg : 0;
      };
      const maxVal = (bone, axis) => {
        const d = armMotionData[bone];
        return d && d[axis] && d[axis].length ? Math.max(...d[axis]) * deg : 0;
      };
      armBaseline = {
        // Shoulder: mean for symmetric swing
        l_shoulder: { x: mean('l_shoulder','x_rot'), y: mean('l_shoulder','y_rot'), z: mean('l_shoulder','z_rot') },
        r_shoulder: { x: mean('r_shoulder','x_rot'), y: mean('r_shoulder','y_rot'), z: mean('r_shoulder','z_rot') },
        // Elbow: min-bend baseline (left=min z_rot, right=max z_rot i.e. closest to 0)
        l_elbow:    { x: first('l_elbow','x_rot'), y: first('l_elbow','y_rot'), z: minVal('l_elbow','z_rot') },
        r_elbow:    { x: first('r_elbow','x_rot'), y: first('r_elbow','y_rot'), z: maxVal('r_elbow','z_rot') },
        // Wrist: same min-bend approach
        l_wrist:    { x: first('l_wrist','x_rot'), y: first('l_wrist','y_rot'), z: minVal('l_wrist','z_rot') },
        r_wrist:    { x: first('r_wrist','x_rot'), y: first('r_wrist','y_rot'), z: maxVal('r_wrist','z_rot') },
      };
    }

    // cage mesh wireframe
    const cgeom = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const carr = new Float32Array(cageRest.length*3);
    for(let i=0;i<cageRest.length;i++){
      carr[i*3]=cageRest[i][0];
      carr[i*3+1]=cageRest[i][1];
      carr[i*3+2]=cageRest[i][2];
    }
    cgeom.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(carr,3));
    const cidx = new Uint32Array(cageFaces.length*3);
    for(let i=0;i<cageFaces.length;i++){
      cidx[i*3]=cageFaces[i][0];
      cidx[i*3+1]=cageFaces[i][1];
      cidx[i*3+2]=cageFaces[i][2];
    }
    cgeom.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(cidx,1));
    cageMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      cgeom,
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x4ecdc4, wireframe:true, transparent:true, opacity:0.5})
    );
    scene.add(cageMesh);

    statusEl.textContent = "Ready.";
  })().catch(err=>{
    console.error(err);
    statusEl.textContent = "Load failed. Make sure you're using a local server (not file://).";
  });

  // ---------------- Animate (real dt from performance.now) ----------------
  let time = 0;
  let lastNow = performance.now();
  let detLogTimer = 0;
  let wasPlaying = false; // track play state transitions for rest-pose reset

  function tick(){
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    const now = performance.now();
    const rawDt = (now - lastNow) / 1000;
    lastNow = now;
    const dt = Math.min(rawDt, 0.05); // clamp to 50ms max

    const isPlaying = $('play').checked && cageRest && (motionData || jointPosData);

    // Reset to rest pose when animation stops (or in placement mode)
    if ((!isPlaying && wasPlaying) || (placementMode && wasPlaying)) {
      if (meshGeo && meshRestPos) {
        const pos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array;
        for (let i = 0; i < meshRestPos.length; i++) pos[i] = meshRestPos[i];
        meshGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
        meshGeo.computeVertexNormals();
        // Reset vertex colors
        if (meshObj) {
          meshObj.material.vertexColors = false;
          if (meshObj.material._savedMap) { meshObj.material.map = meshObj.material._savedMap; delete meshObj.material._savedMap; }
          meshObj.material.needsUpdate = true;
        }
      }
      if (cageRest && cagePos) {
        for (let i = 0; i < cageRest.length; i++) {
          cagePos[i*3] = cageRest[i][0]; cagePos[i*3+1] = cageRest[i][1]; cagePos[i*3+2] = cageRest[i][2];
        }
        updateCageMesh();
      }
    }
    wasPlaying = isPlaying && !placementMode;

    if(isPlaying && !placementMode){
      time += dt * (+$('speed').value);
      driveCageFromMixamo(time);
      deformMesh();
      if(cageMesh){
        cageMesh.visible = $('showCage').checked;
        updateCageMesh();
      }

      // ── Multi-frame diagnostic accumulator ──
      if (_mfDiag && !_mfDiag.done && meshGeo && meshAdjacency && meshRestPos) {
        const pos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array;
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        const { edgeA, edgeB, edgeRestLen, nEdges } = meshAdjacency;
        _mfDiag.frameCount++;
        if (_mfDiag.frameCount === 1) console.log('[MULTI-FRAME] Accumulator started');
        if (_mfDiag.frameCount % 60 === 0) console.log('[MULTI-FRAME] Frame ' + _mfDiag.frameCount + '/' + _mfDiag.targetFrames);

        // (a) Track worst edge stretch per frame
        let frameWorstStretch = 0, frameWorstEdge = -1;
        let frameTotalDev = 0;
        for (let e = 0; e < nEdges; e++) {
          const a = edgeA[e], b = edgeB[e];
          const dx = pos[b*3]-pos[a*3], dy = pos[b*3+1]-pos[a*3+1], dz = pos[b*3+2]-pos[a*3+2];
          const curLen = Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz);
          const rest = edgeRestLen[e];
          if (rest < 1e-8) continue;
          const ratio = curLen / rest;
          const dev = Math.abs(ratio - 1.0);
          frameTotalDev += dev;
          if (ratio > frameWorstStretch) { frameWorstStretch = ratio; frameWorstEdge = e; }
        }
        const frameAvgDev = frameTotalDev / nEdges;
        if (frameWorstStretch > _mfDiag.worstStretch) {
          _mfDiag.worstStretch = frameWorstStretch;
          _mfDiag.worstStretchFrame = _mfDiag.frameCount;
          _mfDiag.worstStretchEdge = frameWorstEdge;
        }
        _mfDiag.totalAvgDev += frameAvgDev;
        if (frameAvgDev > _mfDiag.worstAvgDev) _mfDiag.worstAvgDev = frameAvgDev;

        // (b) Track vertex jitter (acceleration = velocity change between frames)
        // True jitter is rapid direction changes, not smooth fast movement
        if (_mfDiag.prevPos) {
          // Compute current velocities
          if (!_mfDiag.curVel) _mfDiag.curVel = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
          for (let i = 0; i < nMesh * 3; i++) {
            _mfDiag.curVel[i] = pos[i] - _mfDiag.prevPos[i];
          }
          if (_mfDiag.prevVel) {
            // Acceleration = velocity change
            let maxAccel = 0, accelVert = -1, totalAccel = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
              const ax = _mfDiag.curVel[i*3] - _mfDiag.prevVel[i*3];
              const ay = _mfDiag.curVel[i*3+1] - _mfDiag.prevVel[i*3+1];
              const az = _mfDiag.curVel[i*3+2] - _mfDiag.prevVel[i*3+2];
              const accel = Math.sqrt(ax*ax+ay*ay+az*az);
              totalAccel += accel;
              if (accel > maxAccel) { maxAccel = accel; accelVert = i; }
            }
            const avgAccel = totalAccel / nMesh;
            if (maxAccel > _mfDiag.worstJitter) {
              _mfDiag.worstJitter = maxAccel;
              _mfDiag.worstJitterVert = accelVert;
              _mfDiag.worstJitterFrame = _mfDiag.frameCount;
            }
            _mfDiag.totalAvgJitter += avgAccel;
          }
          // Store current velocity as previous for next frame
          if (!_mfDiag.prevVel) _mfDiag.prevVel = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
          _mfDiag.prevVel.set(_mfDiag.curVel);
        }
        _mfDiag.prevPos = pos.slice();

        // (c) Track foot ground contact
        if (meshVertRegions) {
          for (const footRid of [5, 6]) { // l_foot=5, r_foot=6
            let minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
            for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
              const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
              if (!entries) continue;
              let footW = 0;
              for (let k = 0; k < entries.length; k += 2) {
                if (entries[k] === footRid) { footW = entries[k+1]; break; }
              }
              if (footW < 0.5) continue; // only dominant foot verts
              const y = pos[i*3+1];
              if (y < minY) minY = y;
              if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
            }
            if (minY < Infinity) {
              const key = footRid === 5 ? 'lFoot' : 'rFoot';
              if (minY < _mfDiag[key + 'MinY']) _mfDiag[key + 'MinY'] = minY;
              _mfDiag[key + 'TotalMinY'] += minY;
            }
          }
        }

        // (d) Track bone direction errors + angular velocity frame-by-frame
        if (retargetedDirs && fbxFrameDirs) {
          if (!_mfDiag.boneDirData) {
            const bones = ['l_thigh','r_thigh','l_shin','r_shin','l_foot','r_foot',
                           'l_uarm','r_uarm','l_farm','r_farm','torso','head'];
            _mfDiag.boneDirData = {};
            for (const b of bones) {
              _mfDiag.boneDirData[b] = {
                maxAngErr: 0, totalAngErr: 0, worstFrame: 0, count: 0,
                maxAngVel: 0, totalAngVel: 0, maxAngAccel: 0, totalAngAccel: 0,
                prevDir: null, prevAngVel: 0, velCount: 0, accelCount: 0,
                // Per-frame tracking for timeline dump
                frameErrors: [], frameAngVels: []
              };
            }
          }
          const bd = _mfDiag.boneDirData;
          for (const bone of Object.keys(bd)) {
            const ours = retargetedDirs[bone];
            const fbx = fbxFrameDirs[bone];
            if (ours && fbx) {
              // Angular error between our direction and FBX direction (degrees)
              const angErr = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(ours, fbx)))) * 180 / Math.PI;
              bd[bone].totalAngErr += angErr;
              bd[bone].count++;
              bd[bone].frameErrors.push(angErr);
              if (angErr > bd[bone].maxAngErr) {
                bd[bone].maxAngErr = angErr;
                bd[bone].worstFrame = _mfDiag.frameCount;
              }
              // Angular velocity: how fast our direction changes frame-to-frame (deg/frame)
              if (bd[bone].prevDir) {
                const angVel = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(ours, bd[bone].prevDir)))) * 180 / Math.PI;
                bd[bone].totalAngVel += angVel;
                bd[bone].velCount++;
                bd[bone].frameAngVels.push(angVel);
                if (angVel > bd[bone].maxAngVel) bd[bone].maxAngVel = angVel;
                // Angular acceleration: change in angular velocity (deg/frame^2)
                const angAccel = Math.abs(angVel - bd[bone].prevAngVel);
                bd[bone].totalAngAccel += angAccel;
                bd[bone].accelCount++;
                if (angAccel > bd[bone].maxAngAccel) bd[bone].maxAngAccel = angAccel;
                bd[bone].prevAngVel = angVel;
              }
              bd[bone].prevDir = ours.slice();
            }
          }
        }

        // (e) Track joint angle errors per frame (coordinate-agnostic)
        if (retargetedAngles && fbxFrameAngles) {
          if (!_mfDiag.jointAngleData) {
            const joints = ['l_knee','r_knee','l_ankle','r_ankle','l_elbow','r_elbow',
                           'l_hip','r_hip','l_shoulder','r_shoulder'];
            _mfDiag.jointAngleData = {};
            for (const j of joints) {
              _mfDiag.jointAngleData[j] = {
                maxErr: 0, totalErr: 0, count: 0, worstFrame: 0,
                maxVelDiff: 0, totalVelDiff: 0, velCount: 0,
                prevOurs: null, prevFbx: null, frameErrors: []
              };
            }
          }
          const jad = _mfDiag.jointAngleData;
          for (const joint of Object.keys(jad)) {
            const ours = retargetedAngles[joint];
            const fbx = fbxFrameAngles[joint];
            if (ours !== null && fbx !== null) {
              const err = Math.abs(ours - fbx);
              jad[joint].totalErr += err;
